function proceedAfterSuccess(parentId) {
  closeModal();
  enrollmentState = {};
  loginByParentId(parentId);
}

// ============================================
// ⚠️ Modal ยืนยันการยกเลิกลงทะเบียน (แทนที่ confirm เดิม)
// ============================================
function cancelEnrollment() {
  // สร้าง Element แจ้งเตือนลอยทับขึ้นมาอีกชั้น (z-[100] เพื่อให้อยู่บนสุด)
  const confirmModal = document.createElement('div');
  confirmModal.id = 'cancelConfirmModal';
  confirmModal.className = 'fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-60 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4 fade-in';
  
  confirmModal.innerHTML = `
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 text-center transform transition-all scale-100">
      <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-yellow-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 shadow-sm">
        <svg class="w-10 h-10 text-yellow-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z"/>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 mb-2">ต้องการยกเลิกหรือไม่?</h3>
      <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-6">
        ข้อมูลเลขบัตรประชาชนที่บันทึกแล้วจะยังคงอยู่ในระบบ<br>ท่านสามารถเข้ามาลงทะเบียนต่อได้ภายหลัง
      </p>
      <div class="flex gap-3">
        <button onclick="document.getElementById('cancelConfirmModal').remove()" class="flex-1 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 py-2.5 rounded-lg font-medium transition">
          กลับไปทำต่อ
        </button>
        <button onclick="executeCancelEnrollment()" class="flex-1 bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white py-2.5 rounded-lg font-medium shadow transition">
          ยืนยันยกเลิก
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  
  // นำไปแปะไว้ใน body
  document.body.appendChild(confirmModal);
}

// ⚙️ ฟังก์ชันทำงานเมื่อกดยืนยันการยกเลิกจริงๆ
function executeCancelEnrollment() {
  // ลบกล่องแจ้งเตือนออก
  const confirmModal = document.getElementById('cancelConfirmModal');
  if (confirmModal) confirmModal.remove();
  
  // ปิดหน้าต่างลงทะเบียนหลัก และล้างข้อมูล State ทิ้ง
  closeModal();
  enrollmentState = {};
}

// ฟังก์ชันนี้ไม่จำเป็นต้องใช้แล้ว แต่เอาไว้ป้องกัน Error กรณีมีปุ่มไหนเรียก
function startParentEnrollment(studentId) {
    startEnrollmentFlow(studentId);
}


// ============================================
// 🏠 Main App Layout
// ============================================
function renderMainApp() {
  const role = currentUser.role;
  const schoolName = appSettings.schoolName || 'โรงเรียนอิสลาม';
  const appName = appSettings.appName || 'Hifz Tracker Pro';
  const logoUrl = appSettings.logoUrl || '';
  
  const roleLabels = {
    admin: '⚙️ แอดมิน',
    director: '👔 ผู้อำนวยการ',
    teacher: '👩‍🏫 ครูที่ปรึกษา',
    parent: '👨‍👩‍👧 ผู้ปกครอง'
  };
  
  // เมนูตาม Role
  let menuItems = [
    { id: 'dashboard', icon: '📊', label: 'แดชบอร์ด' }
  ];
  
  if (role === 'admin' || role === 'director' || role === 'teacher') {
    menuItems.push({ id: 'students', icon: '👦', label: 'นักเรียน' });
    menuItems.push({ id: 'logs', icon: '📝', label: 'บันทึกการท่อง' });
  }
  
  if (role === 'parent') {
    // ⚡ เปลี่ยนเมนูผู้ปกครองให้วิ่งไปหน้าบันทึกการท่อง (Logs)
    menuItems.push({ id: 'logs', icon: '📝', label: 'บันทึกการท่อง' });
  }
  
  menuItems.push({ id: 'reports', icon: '📈', label: 'รายงาน' });
  
  if (role === 'admin') {
    menuItems.push({ id: 'admin', icon: '⚙️', label: 'จัดการระบบ' });
  }
  
  document.getElementById('app').innerHTML = `
    <div class="min-h-screen flex flex-col">
      <!-- Header -->
      <header class="primary-bg text-white shadow-lg sticky top-0 z-40">
        <div class="container mx-auto px-4 py-3">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
              ${logoUrl ? `<img src="${logoUrl}" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover">` : `
                <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-white bg-opacity-20 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <span class="text-xl">🕌</span>
                </div>
              `}
              <div>
                <h1 class="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold">${appName}</h1>
                <p class="text-xs opacity-90">${schoolName} • เทอม ${appSettings.currentTerm || '1/2569'}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <!-- Profile Button -->
              <button onclick="showProfileModal()" class="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white bg-opacity-20 hover:bg-opacity-30 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition" title="โปรไฟล์">
                ${getProfileAvatar(currentUser, 32)}
                <div class="hidden md:block text-left">
                  <p class="text-xs font-medium">${currentUser.name}</p>
                  <p class="text-[10px] opacity-80">${roleLabels[role] || role}</p>
                </div>
              </button>
              <button onclick="forceRefresh()" title="รีเฟรช"
                class="bg-white bg-opacity-20 hover:bg-opacity-30 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm">
                <svg class="w-5 h-5 inline" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15"/>
                </svg>
              </button>
              <button onclick="logout()" class="bg-white bg-opacity-20 hover:bg-opacity-30 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm">
                <svg class="w-5 h-5 inline" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M17 16l4-4m0 0l-4-4m4 4H7m6 4v1a3 3 0 01-3 3H6a3 3 0 01-3-3V7a3 3 0 013-3h4a3 3 0 013 3v1"/>
                </svg>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>
      
<!-- 🕌 Hadith Bar -->
      <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-50 via-white to-amber-50 border-b border-amber-100 py-2 marquee-container">
              <div class="marquee-content">
                <p dir="rtl" style="font-family: 'Scheherazade New', 'Traditional Arabic', serif; font-size: 20px; color: #1a1a1a; line-height: 1.5; padding: 0 20px;">
                  ${appSettings.hadithArabic || 'خَيْرُكُمْ مَنْ تَعَلَّمَ الْقُرْآنَ وَعَلَّمَهُ'} 
                  ${appSettings.hadithThai ? `<span style="font-family: 'Sarabun', sans-serif; font-size: 14px; color: #666; margin-right: 15px;"> — ${appSettings.hadithThai}</span>` : ''}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
      
      <!-- Navigation -->
      <nav class="bg-white shadow-md sticky top-[64px] z-30">
        <div class="container mx-auto">
          <div class="flex overflow-x-auto">
            ${menuItems.map(item => `
              <button onclick="switchTab('${item.id}')" id="tab-${item.id}" 
                class="nav-tab px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap hover:bg-gray-100 border-b-2 border-transparent">
                <span class="mr-1">${item.icon}</span>${item.label}
              </button>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      </nav>
      
      <!-- Content -->
      <main id="mainContent" class="flex-1 container mx-auto px-4 py-6">
        <!-- Dynamic content -->
      </main>
      
      <!-- Footer -->
      <footer class="bg-gray-800 text-white py-4 mt-8">
        <div class="container mx-auto px-4 text-center text-sm">
          <p>© 2024 ${schoolName} | Powered by ${appName}</p>
          <p class="text-xs opacity-75 mt-1">Developer: KruHambaleeWaji (0643230483)</p>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  `;
  
  switchTab('dashboard');
}

function switchTab(tabId) {
  currentTab = tabId;
  
  // อัปเดต Active Tab
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-tab').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.remove('border-b-2');
    el.style.borderBottomColor = '';
    el.style.color = '';
  });
  
  const activeTab = document.getElementById('tab-' + tabId);
  if (activeTab) {
    activeTab.style.borderBottomColor = 'var(--primary-color)';
    activeTab.style.color = 'var(--primary-color)';
  }
  
  // โหลดเนื้อหา
  switch(tabId) {
    case 'dashboard': renderDashboard(); break;
    case 'students': renderStudents(); break;
    case 'mychildren': renderMyChildren(); break;
    case 'logs': renderLogsTab(); break;
    case 'reports': renderReports(); break;
    case 'admin': renderAdminPanel(); break;
  }
}

// ============================================
// 📊 Dashboard (Cached Version)
// ============================================
// ============================================
// 📊 Dashboard (เวอร์ชัน Supabase - คำนวณฝั่ง Client)
// ============================================
async function renderDashboard() {
  const content = document.getElementById('mainContent');
  const dashKey = `dashboard_${currentUser.userId}_${currentUser.role}`;
  const cachedDash = cacheGet(dashKey);

  if (cachedDash) {
    // โหลดจาก Cache ขึ้นมาโชว์ก่อนทันที
    renderDashboardContent(cachedDash);
    // แอบโหลดข้อมูลใหม่ในพื้นหลัง
    fetchDashboardSupabase(dashKey, true);
    return;
  }

  const loader = showProgressLoader({ estimatedSeconds: 8 });
  fetchDashboardSupabase(dashKey, false, loader);
}

async function fetchDashboardSupabase(cacheKey, silent, loader) {
  try {
    // 1. ดึงข้อมูลนักเรียน (⚡ กรองตาม Role ของผู้ใช้งาน)
    let stdQuery = supabaseClient.from('Students').select('*').eq('isActive', true);
    
    if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') {
      stdQuery = stdQuery.eq('teacherId', currentUser.userId);
    } else if (currentUser.role === 'parent') {
      // 👈 ถ้าเป็นผู้ปกครอง ให้ดึงเฉพาะนักเรียนที่มี parentId ตรงกับตัวเอง
      stdQuery = stdQuery.eq('parentId', currentUser.userId);
    }

    // ดึงตารางพื้นฐานอื่นๆ มาพร้อมกัน
    const [stdReq, tchReq, progReq, surahReq] = await Promise.all([
      stdQuery,
      supabaseClient.from('Teachers').select('*').eq('isActive', true),
      supabaseClient.from('Programs').select('*').eq('isActive', true),
      supabaseClient.from('Surahs').select('*')
    ]);

    if (stdReq.error) throw stdReq.error;
    const students = stdReq.data || [];

    // 2. ดึงข้อมูล Logs (⚡ กรองให้ตรงกับสิทธิ์การเข้าถึง)
    let logQuery = supabaseClient.from('Logs').select('*').order('date', { ascending: false });
    
    if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') {
      logQuery = logQuery.eq('teacherId', currentUser.userId);
    } else if (currentUser.role === 'parent') {
      // 👈 ถ้าเป็นผู้ปกครอง ให้ดึง Logs เฉพาะของลูกตัวเองเท่านั้น
      const sIds = students.map(s => s.studentId);
      if (sIds.length > 0) {
        logQuery = logQuery.in('studentId', sIds);
      } else {
        // ถ้าบังเอิญยังไม่มีข้อมูลลูกผูกไว้ ให้ล็อกไว้ไม่ให้ดึงข้อมูลใครมาเลย
        logQuery = logQuery.eq('studentId', 'NONE'); 
      }
    }

    const logReq = await logQuery;
    if (logReq.error) throw logReq.error;

    const teachers = tchReq.data || [];
    const programs = progReq.data || [];
    const logs = logReq.data || [];
    const surahs = surahReq.data || [];

    // เซฟลง Global State เผื่อแท็บอื่นเรียกใช้งาน
    allStudents = students; allTeachers = teachers; allPrograms = programs; allSurahs = surahs; allLogs = logs;

    // 3. 🧮 เริ่มต้นการคำนวณสถิติ (Data Crunching)
    const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    const todayLogs = logs.filter(l => (l.date || '').startsWith(todayStr));

    let dashData = {
      totalStudents: students.length,
      todayLogs: todayLogs.length,
      needReview: logs.filter(l => l.status === 'NEED_REVIEW').length,
      totalTeachers: teachers.filter(t => t.role === 'teacher').length,
      classProgress: [], classRanking: [], logTypeStats: {}, teacherStatus: [], recentLogs: []
    };

    // 📝 ประวัติการท่องล่าสุด (Recent Logs)
    dashData.recentLogs = logs.slice(0, 10).map(l => {
      const st = students.find(s => s.studentId === l.studentId);
      return { ...l, studentName: st ? `${st.firstName} ${st.lastName}` : l.studentId };
    });

    // 📊 สถิติประเภทการท่อง (7 วันล่าสุด)
    const sevenDaysAgo = new Date();
    sevenDaysAgo.setDate(sevenDaysAgo.getDate() - 7);
    logs.filter(l => new Date(l.date || '') >= sevenDaysAgo).forEach(l => {
      dashData.logTypeStats[l.logType] = (dashData.logTypeStats[l.logType] || 0) + 1;
    });

    // 👩‍🏫 สถานะการบันทึกของครู (Admin/Director)
    if (currentUser.role === 'admin' || currentUser.role === 'director') {
      const teacherList = teachers.filter(t => t.role === 'teacher' && t.classRoom && t.classRoom !== '-');
      dashData.teacherStatus = teacherList.map(t => {
        const classStds = students.filter(s => s.classRoom === t.classRoom);
        const classLogsToday = todayLogs.filter(l => classStds.some(s => s.studentId === l.studentId));
        const loggedStdIds = new Set(classLogsToday.map(l => l.studentId));
        const pct = classStds.length ? Math.round((loggedStdIds.size / classStds.length) * 100) : 0;
        return {
          teacherName: `${t.firstName} ${t.lastName}`,
          classRoom: t.classRoom,
          status: pct === 100 ? 'complete' : (pct > 0 ? 'partial' : 'none'),
          loggedToday: loggedStdIds.size, totalStudents: classStds.length, percentage: pct
        };
      });
    }

    // 📚 ความคืบหน้าแต่ละห้อง (Class Progress)
    const surahsByJuz = {};
    surahs.forEach(s => {
      if (!surahsByJuz[s.juz]) surahsByJuz[s.juz] = [];
      surahsByJuz[s.juz].push(s);
    });

    const classes = [...new Set(students.map(s => s.classRoom).filter(c => c && c !== '-'))];
    dashData.classProgress = classes.map(c => {
      const classStds = students.filter(s => s.classRoom === c);
      const t = teachers.find(th => th.classRoom === c && th.role === 'teacher');
      let totalCompletedJuz = 0, totalTargetJuz = 0;

      classStds.forEach(st => {
        const p = programs.find(pg => pg.programId === st.programId);
        if (p) totalTargetJuz += (Number(p.targetJuzPerTerm) || 0);

        const stLogs = logs.filter(l => l.studentId === st.studentId && l.status === 'PASS');
        const passedSurahs = new Set(stLogs.map(l => Number(l.surahNumber)));
        let completedJuz = 0;

        for (let juz = 1; juz <= 30; juz++) {
          const sInJuz = surahsByJuz[juz] || [];
          if (sInJuz.length > 0 && sInJuz.every(s => passedSurahs.has(Number(s.number)))) completedJuz++;
        }
        totalCompletedJuz += completedJuz;
      });

      const avgCompleted = classStds.length ? (totalCompletedJuz / classStds.length).toFixed(1) : 0;
      const avgTarget = classStds.length ? (totalTargetJuz / classStds.length).toFixed(1) : 0;
      const progPct = avgTarget > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((avgCompleted / avgTarget) * 100)) : 0;
      
      const classLogsToday = todayLogs.filter(l => classStds.some(s => s.studentId === l.studentId));
      const loggedStdIds = new Set(classLogsToday.map(l => l.studentId));

      return {
        className: c, totalStudents: classStds.length,
        teacherName: t ? `${t.firstName} ${t.lastName}` : '-',
        avgCompletedJuz: parseFloat(avgCompleted), avgTargetJuz: parseFloat(avgTarget),
        progressPercentage: progPct, loggedToday: loggedStdIds.size,
        todayPercentage: classStds.length ? Math.round((loggedStdIds.size / classStds.length) * 100) : 0
      };
    });

    // กรองแสดงเฉพาะห้องตัวเองถ้าเป็นครู
    if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') {
      const myClass = teachers.find(t => t.teacherId === currentUser.userId)?.classRoom;
      dashData.classProgress = dashData.classProgress.filter(c => c.className === myClass);
    }

    dashData.classRanking = [...dashData.classProgress].sort((a,b) => b.progressPercentage - a.progressPercentage);

    // 4. ส่งข้อมูลไปวาด UI
    const response = { success: true, data: dashData };
    cacheSet(cacheKey, response);

    if (loader) {
      loader.finish();
      setTimeout(() => { if (currentTab === 'dashboard') renderDashboardContent(response); }, 500);
    } else {
      if (!silent && currentTab === 'dashboard') renderDashboardContent(response);
    }

  } catch (error) {
    if (loader) loader.fail('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + error.message);
    else if (!silent) showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + error.message, 'error');
    console.error(error);
  }
}

// 🆕 Separated render function (reuse)
function renderDashboardContent(response) {
  const data = response.data;
  const role = currentUser.role;
  const content = document.getElementById('mainContent');  // ⬅️ เพิ่มบรรทัดนี้
  
  let html = `
        <div class="mb-6">
          <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">แดชบอร์ด</h2>
          <p class="text-gray-600 text-sm">ภาพรวมระบบ • ${formatDate(new Date())}</p>
        </div>
        
        <!-- Stats Cards -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-6">
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-5">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 bg-blue-100 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center">
                <svg class="w-6 h-6 text-blue-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M17 20h5v-2a3 3 0 00-5.356-1.857M17 20H7m10 0v-2c0-.656-.126-1.283-.356-1.857M7 20H2v-2a3 3 0 015.356-1.857M7 20v-2c0-.656.126-1.283.356-1.857m0 0a5.002 5.002 0 019.288 0M15 7a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0zm6 3a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0zM7 10a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0z"/></svg>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">${data.totalStudents}</p>
            <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">นักเรียน</p>
          </div>
          
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-5">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 bg-green-100 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center">
                <svg class="w-6 h-6 text-green-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"/></svg>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">${data.todayLogs}</p>
            <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">บันทึกวันนี้</p>
          </div>
          
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-5">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 bg-yellow-100 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center">
                <svg class="w-6 h-6 text-yellow-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z"/></svg>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">${data.needReview}</p>
            <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">ต้องทบทวน</p>
          </div>
          
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-5">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 bg-purple-100 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center">
                <svg class="w-6 h-6 text-purple-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5H7a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2h10a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2h-2M9 5a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2M9 5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2"/></svg>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">${data.totalTeachers || 0}</p>
            <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">ครู</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      
      // 🆕 Class Progress Cards (Admin/Director + Teacher)
      if ((role === 'director' || role === 'admin' || role === 'teacher') && data.classProgress && data.classProgress.length > 0) {
        const cardTitle = role === 'teacher' ? '📚 ความคืบหน้าห้องของคุณ' : '📚 ความคืบหน้าแต่ละชั้น';
        
        html += `
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow mb-6">
            <div class="p-5 border-b flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 class="font-bold text-gray-800">${cardTitle}</h3>
              <span class="text-xs text-gray-500">${data.classProgress.length} ห้อง</span>
            </div>
            <div class="p-5 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              ${data.classProgress.map(c => {
                const progressColor = c.progressPercentage >= 80 ? 'bg-green-500' :
                                      c.progressPercentage >= 50 ? 'bg-yellow-500' :
                                      c.progressPercentage >= 20 ? 'bg-orange-500' : 'bg-red-500';
                const bgGradient = c.progressPercentage >= 80 ? 'from-green-50 to-green-100' :
                                   c.progressPercentage >= 50 ? 'from-yellow-50 to-yellow-100' :
                                   'from-gray-50 to-gray-100';
                
                const todayColor = c.todayPercentage === 100 ? 'text-green-600' :
                                   c.todayPercentage > 0 ? 'text-yellow-600' : 'text-red-600';
                
                return `
                  <div class="bg-gradient-to-br ${bgGradient} border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 hover:shadow-lg transition">
                    <!-- Class Name -->
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                      <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-800">${c.className}</h4>
                      <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-white">
                        ${c.totalStudents} คน
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    
                    <!-- Teacher -->
                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-600 mb-3">
                      👩‍🏫 ${c.teacherName}
                    </p>
                    
                    <!-- Progress Bar -->
                    <div class="mb-3">
                      <div class="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
                        <span class="font-medium text-gray-700">ยุซเฉลี่ย</span>
                        <span class="font-bold ${progressColor.replace('bg-', 'text-')}">
                          ${c.avgCompletedJuz}/${c.avgTargetJuz}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div class="w-full bg-white bg-opacity-60 rounded-full h-2.5">
                        <div class="${progressColor} h-2.5 rounded-full transition-all" 
                          style="width: ${c.progressPercentage}%"></div>
                      </div>
                      <p class="text-xs text-right mt-1 font-bold ${progressColor.replace('bg-', 'text-')}">
                        ${c.progressPercentage}%
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    
                    <!-- Today Stats -->
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-gray-300">
                      <span class="text-xs text-gray-600">📝 บันทึกวันนี้</span>
                      <span class="text-sm font-bold ${todayColor}">
                        ${c.loggedToday}/${c.totalStudents} (${c.todayPercentage}%)
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                `;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }
      
      // 🆕 Class Ranking (Admin/Director only)
      if ((role === 'director' || role === 'admin') && data.classRanking && data.classRanking.length > 0) {
        const topClasses = data.classRanking.slice(0, 10);
        
        html += `
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow mb-6">
            <div class="p-5 border-b">
              <h3 class="font-bold text-gray-800">🏆 อันดับความก้าวหน้าของชั้น</h3>
              <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">เรียงตามเปอร์เซ็นต์ความสำเร็จ</p>
            </div>
            <div class="overflow-x-auto">
              <table class="w-full">
                <thead class="bg-gray-50">
                  <tr>
                    <th class="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-600 uppercase">อันดับ</th>
                    <th class="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-600 uppercase">ชั้นเรียน</th>
                    <th class="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-600 uppercase hidden md:table-cell">ครูที่ปรึกษา</th>
                    <th class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-600 uppercase">นักเรียน</th>
                    <th class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-600 uppercase">ยุซเฉลี่ย</th>
                    <th class="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-600 uppercase">Progress</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y">
                  ${topClasses.map((c, index) => {
                    const rank = index + 1;
                    let rankBadge = rank;
                    let rankClass = 'text-gray-600';
                    
                    if (rank === 1) { rankBadge = '🥇'; rankClass = 'text-yellow-600 text-xl'; }
                    else if (rank === 2) { rankBadge = '🥈'; rankClass = 'text-gray-500 text-xl'; }
                    else if (rank === 3) { rankBadge = '🥉'; rankClass = 'text-orange-600 text-xl'; }
                    
                    const barColor = c.progressPercentage >= 80 ? 'bg-green-500' :
                                     c.progressPercentage >= 50 ? 'bg-yellow-500' : 'bg-red-500';
                    
                    return `
                      <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50">
                        <td class="px-4 py-3 font-bold ${rankClass}">${rankBadge}</td>
                        <td class="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-800">${c.className}</td>
                        <td class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-600 hidden md:table-cell">${c.teacherName}</td>
                        <td class="px-4 py-3 text-center text-sm">${c.totalStudents}</td>
                        <td class="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                          <span class="font-bold ${barColor.replace('bg-', 'text-')}">${c.avgCompletedJuz}</span>
                          <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">/${c.avgTargetJuz}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="px-4 py-3">
                          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <div class="flex-1 bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2 min-w-[80px]">
                              <div class="${barColor} h-2 rounded-full transition-all" 
                                style="width: ${c.progressPercentage}%"></div>
                            </div>
                            <span class="text-xs font-bold w-10 text-right">${c.progressPercentage}%</span>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }
      
      // 🆕 Log Types Chart (7 วันล่าสุด)
      if (data.logTypeStats) {
        const types = [
          { key: 'سبق', label: 'ท่องจำ', color: 'bg-blue-500', icon: '📖' },
          { key: 'سبقي', label: 'ทบทวนใหม่', color: 'bg-purple-500', icon: '🔄' },
          { key: 'منزل', label: 'ทบทวนเก่า', color: 'bg-indigo-500', icon: '📚' },
          { key: 'نزيرة', label: 'นาซีเราะห์', color: 'bg-pink-500', icon: '👁️' },
          { key: 'قراءتي', label: 'กีรออาตี', color: 'bg-teal-500', icon: '🔤' }
        ];
        
        const maxCount = Math.max(...types.map(t => data.logTypeStats[t.key] || 0), 1);
        
        html += `
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow mb-6">
            <div class="p-5 border-b">
              <h3 class="font-bold text-gray-800">📊 สถิติการบันทึก 7 วันล่าสุด</h3>
              <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">แยกตามประเภทการท่อง</p>
            </div>
            <div class="p-5 space-y-3">
              ${types.map(t => {
                const count = data.logTypeStats[t.key] || 0;
                const percent = Math.round((count / maxCount) * 100);
                return `
                  <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="w-32 flex-shrink-0">
                      <p class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">${t.icon} ${t.label}</p>
                      <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 arabic" dir="rtl" style="font-family: 'Scheherazade New', serif;">${t.key}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex-1 bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-6 relative overflow-hidden">
                      <div class="${t.color} h-full rounded-full transition-all flex items-center justify-end pr-2" 
                        style="width: ${percent}%; min-width: 40px;">
                        <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${count}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                `;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }
      

      // Director/Admin: Teacher Status
      if ((role === 'director' || role === 'admin') && data.teacherStatus && data.teacherStatus.length > 0) {
        html += `
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow mb-6">
            <div class="p-5 border-b">
              <h3 class="font-bold text-gray-800">📋 สถานะการบันทึกของครู (วันนี้)</h3>
            </div>
            <div class="p-5 space-y-3">
              ${data.teacherStatus.map(t => {
                const statusColors = {
                  complete: 'bg-green-100 text-green-700',
                  partial: 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700',
                  none: 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
                };
                const statusLabels = {
                  complete: '✅ ครบแล้ว',
                  partial: '⏳ กำลังบันทึก',
                  none: '❌ ยังไม่บันทึก'
                };
                return `
                  <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg">
                    <div>
                      <p class="font-medium text-gray-800">${t.teacherName}</p>
                      <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">${t.classRoom}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-right">
                      <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${statusColors[t.status]}">
                        ${statusLabels[t.status]}
                      </span>
                      <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mt-1">${t.loggedToday}/${t.totalStudents} (${t.percentage}%)</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                `;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }
      
      // Recent Logs
      html += `
        <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow">
          <div class="p-5 border-b">
            <h3 class="font-bold text-gray-800">📝 บันทึกล่าสุด</h3>
          </div>
          <div class="divide-y">
            ${data.recentLogs.length === 0 ? 
              '<p class="p-8 text-center text-gray-500">ยังไม่มีบันทึก</p>' :
              data.recentLogs.map(log => {
                const typeInfo = getLogTypeInfo(log.logType);
                return `
                  <div class="p-4 hover:bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div class="flex-1">
                      <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                        <span class="font-medium text-gray-800">${log.studentName}</span>
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs ${typeInfo.color}">${typeInfo.label}</span>
                      </div>
                      <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">
                        ${log.surahName ? `ยุซ ${log.juzNumber} - ${log.surahName}` : '-'}
                        ${log.ayah ? ` • ${log.ayah}` : ''}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-right">
                      ${log.score > 0 ? `<p class="text-sm">⭐ ${log.score}/5</p>` : ''}
                      <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">${formatDateTime(log.date)}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                `;
              }).join('')
            }
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
      
      content.innerHTML = html;
    }

// ============================================
// 👦 Students Tab
// ============================================
function renderStudents() {
  const content = document.getElementById('mainContent');
  
  // Cache key แยกตาม role
  const cacheKey = currentUser.role === 'teacher' ? 
    `students_teacher_${currentUser.userId}` : 'students_all';
  
  // ลองใช้ cache ก่อน
  const cached = cacheGet(cacheKey);
  const cachedPrograms = cacheGet('programs');
  const cachedTeachers = cacheGet('teachers');
  const cachedParents = cacheGet('parents');
  
  if (cached && cachedPrograms && cachedTeachers && cachedParents) {
    // ใช้ cache ทั้งหมด → แสดงทันที
    allStudents = cached.students;
    allPrograms = cachedPrograms.programs;
    allTeachers = cachedTeachers.teachers;
    allParents = cachedParents.parents;
    displayStudents();
    
    // Background refresh (silent)
    refreshStudentsData(cacheKey, true);
    return;
  }
  
// ไม่มี cache ครบ → ใช้ progress loader
const loader = showProgressLoader({ estimatedSeconds: 8 });
refreshStudentsData(cacheKey, false, loader);
}

// ==========================================
// 👦 โหลดข้อมูลนักเรียน (เวอร์ชัน Supabase)
// ==========================================
async function refreshStudentsData(cacheKey, silent, loader) {
  try {
    // 1. ดึงข้อมูลนักเรียน (ถ้าเป็นครู ดึงเฉพาะห้องตัวเอง, ถ้าแอดมิน/ผอ. ดึงทั้งหมด)
    let query = supabaseClient.from('Students').select('*').eq('isActive', true);
    if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') {
      query = query.eq('teacherId', currentUser.userId);
    }
    const { data: students, error: err1 } = await query;
    if (err1) throw err1;
    allStudents = students || [];

    // 2. ดึงข้อมูลโปรแกรม, ครู, และผู้ปกครอง (ดึงพร้อมกัน 3 ตารางเพื่อความเร็วสูงสุด ⚡)
    const [progRes, teachRes, parentRes] = await Promise.all([
      supabaseClient.from('Programs').select('*').eq('isActive', true),
      supabaseClient.from('Teachers').select('*').eq('isActive', true),
      supabaseClient.from('Parents').select('*').eq('isActive', true)
    ]);

    if (progRes.error) throw progRes.error;
    if (teachRes.error) throw teachRes.error;
    if (parentRes.error) throw parentRes.error;

    allPrograms = progRes.data || [];
    allTeachers = teachRes.data || [];
    allParents = parentRes.data || [];

    // 3. อัปเดต Cache ของระบบ
    cacheSet(cacheKey, { success: true, students: allStudents });
    cacheSet('programs', { success: true, programs: allPrograms });
    cacheSet('teachers', { success: true, teachers: allTeachers });
    cacheSet('parents', { success: true, parents: allParents });

    // 4. สั่งให้หน้าเว็บนำข้อมูลมาสร้างตาราง/การ์ดแสดงผล
    if (loader) {
      loader.finish();
      setTimeout(function() {
        if (currentTab === 'students' || currentTab === 'mychildren') displayStudents();
      }, 500);
    } else {
      if (!silent || currentTab === 'students' || currentTab === 'mychildren') displayStudents();
    }

  } catch (error) {
    if (loader) loader.fail('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + error.message);
    else if (!silent) showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + error.message, 'error');
    console.error("Fetch Data Error: ", error);
  }
}

// 🌐 Students View State
let studentViewMode = localStorage.getItem('hifzStudentView') || 'card';
let studentSearchQuery = '';
let studentClassFilter = localStorage.getItem('hifzStudentFilter') || '';

function displayStudents() {
  const canManage = currentUser.role === 'admin' || currentUser.role === 'director' || currentUser.role === 'teacher';
  const canPrint = currentUser.role !== 'parent';
  
  // กรองข้อมูล
  let filtered = allStudents;
  
  if (studentClassFilter) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(s => s.classRoom === studentClassFilter);
  }
  
  if (studentSearchQuery) {
    const q = studentSearchQuery.toLowerCase();
    filtered = filtered.filter(s => 
      (s.firstName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (s.lastName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (s.studentId || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }
  
  // ดึงรายการห้อง
  const uniqueClasses = [...new Set(allStudents.map(s => s.classRoom).filter(c => c))].sort();
  
  const html = `
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-3">
      <div>
        <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">นักเรียน</h2>
        <p class="text-gray-600 text-sm">
          แสดง ${filtered.length} / ${allStudents.length} คน
          ${studentClassFilter ? ` • ห้อง: <strong>${studentClassFilter}</strong>` : ''}
          ${studentSearchQuery ? ` • ค้นหา: "<strong>${studentSearchQuery}</strong>"` : ''}
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
        ${canPrint ? `
          <button onclick="showBulkPrintModal()" class="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium shadow text-sm">
            🖨️ พิมพ์ทั้งห้อง
          </button>
        ` : ''}
        ${canManage ? `
          <button onclick="showStudentModal()" class="primary-bg primary-hover text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium shadow text-sm">
            + เพิ่มนักเรียน
          </button>
        ` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Toolbar: Search + Filter + View Toggle -->
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4 mb-4">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
        
        <!-- Search -->
        <div class="flex-1 min-w-[200px] relative">
          <svg class="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z"/>
          </svg>
          <input type="text" id="studentSearch" placeholder="🔍 ค้นหาชื่อ หรือ รหัสนักเรียน..." 
            value="${studentSearchQuery}"
            class="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600 text-sm">
        </div>
        
        <!-- Class Filter -->
        <select id="studentClassFilter" 
          class="px-4 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600 text-sm min-w-[150px]">
          <option value="">🏫 ทุกห้อง</option>
          ${uniqueClasses.map(c => {
            const count = allStudents.filter(s => s.classRoom === c).length;
            return `<option value="${c}" ${studentClassFilter === c ? 'selected' : ''}>${c} (${count})</option>`;
          }).join('')}
        </select>
        
        <!-- View Toggle -->
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div class="bg-gray-100 rounded-lg p-1 flex">
            <button onclick="switchStudentView('card')" 
              class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium transition ${studentViewMode === 'card' ? 'bg-white shadow primary-text' : 'text-gray-600'}">
              🎴 Card
            </button>
            <button onclick="switchStudentView('table')" 
              class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium transition ${studentViewMode === 'table' ? 'bg-white shadow primary-text' : 'text-gray-600'}">
              📋 Table
            </button>
          </div>
          
          ${canManage ? `
          <button onclick="autoMarkAbsentForToday()" class="bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 border border-red-200 text-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm" title="เช็คขาดนักเรียนที่เหลือของวันนี้">
            <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path></svg>
            เช็คขาด (วันนี้)
          </button>
          ` : ''}
        </div>
        
        <!-- Clear Filter -->
        ${studentClassFilter || studentSearchQuery ? `
          <button onclick="clearStudentFilters()" class="text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 text-sm font-medium">
            ✕ ล้างตัวกรอง
          </button>
        ` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Content Area -->
    <div id="studentsContent">
      ${filtered.length === 0 ? renderNoStudents() : 
        studentViewMode === 'card' ? renderStudentsCard(filtered, canManage) : renderStudentsTable(filtered, canManage)
      }
    </div>
  `;
  
  document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = html;
  
  // Event Listeners
  const searchEl = document.getElementById('studentSearch');
  if (searchEl) {
    searchEl.addEventListener('input', debounce(function() {
      studentSearchQuery = this.value;
      displayStudents();
      // Refocus search after re-render
      setTimeout(() => {
        const newSearch = document.getElementById('studentSearch');
        if (newSearch) {
          newSearch.focus();
          newSearch.setSelectionRange(newSearch.value.length, newSearch.value.length);
        }
      }, 0);
    }, 300));
  }
  
  const filterEl = document.getElementById('studentClassFilter');
  if (filterEl) {
    filterEl.addEventListener('change', function() {
      studentClassFilter = this.value;
      localStorage.setItem('hifzStudentFilter', this.value);
      displayStudents();
    });
  }
}

function renderNoStudents() {
  if (studentSearchQuery || studentClassFilter) {
    return `
      <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-12 text-center">
        <p class="text-4xl mb-3">🔍</p>
        <p class="text-gray-600 font-medium mb-1">ไม่พบนักเรียนที่ตรงกับเงื่อนไข</p>
        <p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">ลองเปลี่ยนคำค้นหาหรือตัวกรอง</p>
        <button onclick="clearStudentFilters()" class="text-blue-600 hover:underline text-sm">
          ล้างตัวกรองทั้งหมด
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  return '<div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-12 text-center text-gray-500">ยังไม่มีนักเรียน</div>';
}

function renderStudentsCard(students, canManage) {
  return `
    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
      ${students.map(s => {
        const program = allPrograms.find(p => p.programId === s.programId);
        return `
          <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow hover:shadow-lg transition p-5">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
              ${s.photoUrl ? 
                `<img src="${s.photoUrl}" class="w-14 h-14 rounded-full object-cover border-2 border-gray-200" 
                  onerror="this.style.display='none'; this.nextElementSibling.style.display='flex';">
                 <div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-full primary-bg flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-lg" style="display:none;">
                   ${s.firstName.charAt(0)}
                 </div>` :
                `<div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-full primary-bg flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-lg">
                  ${s.firstName.charAt(0)}
                </div>`
              }
              <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <p class="font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">${s.firstName} ${s.lastName}</p>
                <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">${s.studentId}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="space-y-1 text-sm text-gray-600 mb-4">
              <p>📚 ${s.classRoom}</p>
              <p>🎯 ${program ? program.programName : s.programId}</p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="flex gap-2">
              <button onclick="viewStudentDetail('${s.studentId}')" class="flex-1 primary-bg primary-hover text-white py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium">
                ดูข้อมูล
              </button>
              ${canManage ? `
                <button onclick="showLogModal('${s.studentId}')" class="flex-1 bg-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-600 text-white py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium">
                  บันทึก
                </button>
              ` : ''}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('')}
    </div>
  `;
}

function renderStudentsTable(students, canManage) {
  return `
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
      <div class="overflow-x-auto">
        <table class="w-full">
          <thead class="primary-bg text-white">
            <tr>
              <th class="px-3 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase">รูป</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase">รหัส</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase">ชื่อ-สกุล</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase">ห้อง</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase hidden md:table-cell">โปรแกรม</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium uppercase">การทำงาน</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

            ${students.map(s => {
              const program = allPrograms.find(p => p.programId === s.programId);
              return `

                  <td class="px-3 py-3">

                      `<img src="${s.photoUrl}" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover border"
