// ============================================
// 🖨️ เปิด Modal พิมพ์รายงาน (ระบบ Supabase)
// ============================================
async function showReportsPrintModal() {
  if (allStudents && allStudents.length > 0) {
    showBulkPrintModal();
    return;
  }

  const cacheKey = currentUser.role === 'teacher' ? `students_teacher_${currentUser.userId}` : 'students_all';
  
  showLoading('กำลังเตรียมข้อมูลนักเรียน...');
  try {
    let query = supabaseClient.from('Students').select('*').eq('isActive', true);
    if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') {
      query = query.eq('teacherId', currentUser.userId);
    }
    const { data: students, error } = await query;
    if (error) throw error;
    
    allStudents = students || [];
    cacheSet(cacheKey, { success: true, students: allStudents });
    hideLoading();
    showBulkPrintModal();
  } catch (error) {
    hideLoading();
    showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาดในการโหลดข้อมูลนักเรียน: ' + error.message, 'error');
  }
}

// ============================================
// 🖨️ พิมพ์รายงานรายบุคคล (ระบบ Supabase)
// ============================================
async function generateReport(studentId, type) {
  closeModal();
  showLoading('กำลังเตรียมรายงาน...');
  
  try {
    const reportData = await fetchReportFromSupabase(studentId);
    const teacher = allTeachers.find(t => t.teacherId === reportData.student.teacherId) || null;
    reportData.teacher = teacher;

    hideLoading();
    
    const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
    if (!printWindow) {
      showToast('กรุณาอนุญาต Popup ในเบราว์เซอร์', 'warning');
      return;
    }
    
    const html = buildReportHTML(reportData, type);
    printWindow.document.write(html);
    printWindow.document.close();
    
  } catch (error) {
    hideLoading();
    showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + error.message, 'error');
  }
}

// ============================================
// 📄 Report HTML Builder (Print Fixed)
// ============================================
function buildReportHTML(r, type) {
  const s = r.student;
  const p = r.program;
  const teacher = r.teacher;
  
  const schoolName = appSettings.schoolName || 'โรงเรียน';
  const schoolNameEn = appSettings.schoolNameEn || '';
  const schoolNameArabic = appSettings.schoolNameArabic || '';
  const logoUrl = appSettings.logoUrl || '';
  const coverImageUrl = appSettings.coverImageUrl || ''; 
  const hadithArabic = appSettings.hadithArabic || 'خَيْرُكُمْ مَنْ تَعَلَّمَ الْقُرْآنَ وَعَلَّمَهُ';
  const hadithEnglish = appSettings.hadithEnglish || 'Verily the most superior amongst you (Muslims) are those who learn the Quran & teach it.';
  const reportTitle = appSettings.reportTitle || 'แบบบันทึกการท่องจำอัลกุรอาน';
  const programSubtitle = appSettings.programSubtitle || 'Al-Quran Program';
  const currentTerm = appSettings.currentTerm || '1/2569';
  const currentYear = currentTerm.split('/')[1] || '2569';
  
  const baseCSS = `
    * { box-sizing: border-box; }
    body { font-family: 'Sarabun', sans-serif; color: #000; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.3; background: #fff; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    @page { size: A4; margin: 10mm; }
    .page { page-break-after: always; min-height: 275mm; padding: 10px; position: relative; }
    .page:last-child { page-break-after: auto; }
    .arabic { font-family: 'Traditional Arabic', 'Scheherazade New', serif; direction: rtl; }
    table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse !important; margin-bottom: 10px; }
    th, td { border: 1px solid #000 !important; padding: 3px 5px; text-align: center; }
    th { font-weight: bold; }
    @media print {
      body { -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact !important; print-color-adjust: exact !important; }
      .page { box-shadow: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    }
  `;
  
  let content = '';
  if (type === 'full' || type === 'cover') content += buildCoverPage(s, p, teacher, schoolName, schoolNameEn, schoolNameArabic, logoUrl, coverImageUrl, hadithArabic, hadithEnglish, reportTitle, programSubtitle, currentYear);
  if (type === 'full' || type === 'daily') content += buildDailyLogPage(r, schoolName);
  if (type === 'full' || type === 'summary') content += buildSummaryPage(r, schoolName, currentTerm);
  
  return `
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="th">
    <head>
      <meta charset="UTF-8">
      <title>รายงาน - ${s.firstName} ${s.lastName}</title>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Sarabun:wght@300;400;600;700&family=Scheherazade+New:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
      <style>${baseCSS}</style>
    </head>
    <body>
      ${content}
      <script>
        window.onload = function() { setTimeout(function() { window.print(); }, 800); };
      <\/script>
    </body>
    </html>
  `;
}

// ============================================
// 📄 Report HTML Builder (รายบุคคล)
// ============================================
function buildReportHTML(r, type) {
  const s = r.student;
  const p = r.program;
  const teacher = r.teacher;

  const schoolName       = appSettings.schoolName       || 'โรงเรียน';
  const schoolNameEn     = appSettings.schoolNameEn     || '';
  const schoolNameArabic = appSettings.schoolNameArabic || '';
  const logoUrl          = appSettings.logoUrl          || '';
  const hadithArabic     = appSettings.hadithArabic     || 'خَيْرُكُمْ مَنْ تَعَلَّمَ الْقُرْآنَ وَعَلَّمَهُ';
  const hadithEnglish    = appSettings.hadithEnglish    || 'Verily the most superior amongst you (Muslims) are those who learn the Quran & teach it.';
  const reportTitle       = appSettings.reportTitle       || 'แบบบันทึกการท่องจำอัลกุรอาน';
  const reportTitleArabic = appSettings.reportTitleArabic || 'جاتتن حفظ القرآن';
  const programSubtitle   = appSettings.programSubtitle   || 'Al-Quran Program';
  const currentTerm = appSettings.currentTerm || '1/2569';
  const currentYear = currentTerm.split('/')[1] || '2569';

  // 🆕 Cover icon: แอดมินเลือกได้ว่าจะใช้อีโมจิ, รูปภาพ, หรือปล่อย default SVG
  const coverConfig = {
    type:  appSettings.coverIconType  || 'image',
    emoji: appSettings.coverIconEmoji || '📖',
    url:   appSettings.coverImageUrl  || ''
  };

  const baseCSS = `
    * { box-sizing: border-box; }
    body { font-family: 'Sarabun', sans-serif; color: #000; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.3; background: #fff; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    @page { size: A4; margin: 10mm; }

    /* บังคับตัดหน้ากระดาษ */
    .page { page-break-after: always; break-after: page; min-height: 275mm; padding: 10px; position: relative; }
    .page-summary { page-break-before: always; break-before: page; }
    .page:last-child { page-break-after: auto; break-after: auto; }

    .arabic { font-family: 'Scheherazade New', 'Traditional Arabic', serif; direction: rtl; }
    table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse !important; margin-bottom: 10px; }
    th, td { border: 1px solid #000 !important; padding: 3px 5px; text-align: center; vertical-align: middle; }
    th { font-weight: bold; }

    .center-text { text-align: center !important; font-weight: bold; }

    @media print {
      body { -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact !important; print-color-adjust: exact !important; }
      .page { box-shadow: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    }

    /* ========== Notebook lined paper (บันทึกเพิ่มเติม) ========== */
    .notebook-paper {
      border: 1px solid #000;
      padding: 0;
      min-height: 240mm;
      background: #fff;
    }
    .notebook-line {
      border-bottom: 1px solid #aaa;
      padding: 6px 12px 6px 48px;
      min-height: 28px;
      font-size: 14px;
      line-height: 1.4;
      word-wrap: break-word;
      background: linear-gradient(to right,
        transparent 0, transparent 38px,
        #c00 38px, #c00 39px,
        transparent 39px);
    }
    .notebook-line:first-child {
      border-top: 2px solid #c00;
    }
  `;

  let content = '';

  if (type === 'full' || type === 'cover') {
    content += buildCoverPage(
      s, p, teacher,
      schoolName, schoolNameEn, schoolNameArabic,
      logoUrl, coverConfig,
      hadithArabic, hadithEnglish,
      reportTitle, reportTitleArabic, programSubtitle,
      currentYear
    );
  }
  if (type === 'full' || type === 'daily') {
    content += buildDailyLogPage(r, schoolName);
  }
  if (type === 'full' || type === 'summary') {
    content += buildSummaryPage(r, schoolName, currentTerm);
  }
  if (type === 'full' || type === 'notes') {
    content += buildNotesPage(r, schoolName);
  }

  return `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="th">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>รายงาน - ${s.firstName} ${s.lastName}</title>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Sarabun:wght@300;400;600;700&family=Scheherazade+New:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
  <style>${baseCSS}</style>
</head>
<body>
${content}
<script>
  window.onload = function() { setTimeout(function() { window.print(); }, 800); };
<\/script>
</body>
</html>
  `;
}

// ============================================
// 📄 Cover Page (หน้าปก)
// ============================================
function buildCoverPage(s, p, teacher, schoolName, schoolNameEn, schoolNameArabic, logoUrl, coverConfig, hadithArabic, hadithEnglish, reportTitle, reportTitleArabic, programSubtitle, currentYear) {

  const quranStandSvg = `
    <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="#000" stroke-width="1.5" style="width: 100%; max-height: 120px;">
      <path d="M 20 35 L 50 25 L 80 35 L 80 55 L 50 48 L 20 55 Z" fill="#fff"/>
      <line x1="50" y1="25" x2="50" y2="48"/><line x1="30" y1="34" x2="45" y2="30"/><line x1="55" y1="30" x2="70" y2="34"/>
      <line x1="30" y1="40" x2="45" y2="36"/><line x1="55" y1="36" x2="70" y2="40"/><line x1="35" y1="55" x2="25" y2="85"/>
      <line x1="65" y1="55" x2="75" y2="85"/><line x1="50" y1="52" x2="50" y2="85"/><line x1="22" y1="85" x2="78" y2="85"/>
    </svg>
  `;

  // 🆕 เลือกการแสดง icon ตามที่ admin ตั้งไว้
  let iconHtml = quranStandSvg; // default
  if (coverConfig.type === 'emoji' && coverConfig.emoji) {
    iconHtml = `<div style="font-size: 90px; line-height: 1;">${coverConfig.emoji}</div>`;
  } else if (coverConfig.type === 'image' && coverConfig.url) {
    iconHtml = `<img src="${coverConfig.url}" style="max-width: 100%; max-height: 120px;">`;
  }

  return `
<div class="page">
  <div style="border: 4px double #000; padding: 20px; height: 100%; min-height: 270mm; display: flex; flex-direction: column; position: relative;">

    <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 10px;">
      ${logoUrl ? `<img src="${logoUrl}" style="height: 80px; object-fit: contain;">` : '<div style="height:80px;"></div>'}
    </div>

    <div style="border-top: 1.5px solid #000; border-bottom: 1.5px solid #000; padding: 8px 0; text-align: center; margin-bottom: 30px;">
      <div style="font-size: 22px; font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 2px;">${schoolName}</div>
      <div style="font-size: 14px; text-transform: uppercase;">${schoolNameEn}</div>
    </div>

    <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px;">
      <h1 style="font-size: 40px; font-weight: bold; margin: 0 0 15px 0;">${reportTitle}</h1>
      <h1 class="arabic" style="font-size: 38px; font-weight: normal; margin: 0 0 15px 0;">${reportTitleArabic}</h1>
      <h1 style="font-size: 48px; font-family: 'Times New Roman', serif; font-style: italic; font-weight: normal; margin: 0;">${programSubtitle}</h1>
    </div>

    <div style="border: 2px double #000; padding: 15px; margin: 30px 10px; display: flex; align-items: center;">
      <div style="width: 30%; text-align: center;">${iconHtml}</div>
      <div style="width: 70%; text-align: center; padding-left: 10px;">
        <p class="arabic" style="font-size: 28px; font-weight: bold; line-height: 1.6; margin: 0 0 10px 0;">${hadithArabic}</p>
        <p style="font-size: 14px; font-family: 'Times New Roman', serif; font-style: italic; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${hadithEnglish}</p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div style="border: 1.5px solid #000; padding: 20px; position: absolute; bottom: 20px; left: 20px; right: 20px; font-size: 16px; line-height: 2.2;">
      <div style="display: flex;">
        <div style="flex: 1;">ชื่อ <span style="display: inline-block; border-bottom: 1px dotted #000; width: 85%;">${s.firstName || ''}</span></div>
        <div style="flex: 1;">นามสกุล <span style="display: inline-block; border-bottom: 1px dotted #000; width: 80%;">${s.lastName || ''}</span></div>
      </div>
      <div style="display: flex;">
        <div style="flex: 0.8;">เลขที่ <span style="display: inline-block; border-bottom: 1px dotted #000; width: 60%;">&nbsp;</span></div>
        <div style="flex: 0.8;">ชั้น <span style="display: inline-block; border-bottom: 1px dotted #000; width: 60%;">${s.classRoom || ''}</span></div>
        <div style="flex: 1.4;">เลขประจำตัว <span style="display: inline-block; border-bottom: 1px dotted #000; width: 65%;">${s.studentId || ''}</span></div>
      </div>
      <div style="display: flex;">
        <div style="flex: 1;">โปรแกรม <span style="display: inline-block; border-bottom: 1px dotted #000; width: 75%;">${p ? p.programName : ''}</span></div>
        <div style="flex: 1;">ปีการศึกษา <span style="display: inline-block; border-bottom: 1px dotted #000; width: 75%;">${currentYear}</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>

  </div>
</div>
  `;
}

// ============================================
// 📝 Daily Log Page (ตารางบันทึกประจำวันบังคับเต็มหน้า)
// ============================================
function buildDailyLogPage(r, schoolName) {
  const s = r.student;
  const logs = r.allLogs || [];

  const formatDateThai = (dateStr) => {
    if (!dateStr) return '';
    const d = new Date(dateStr);
    return `${d.getDate()}/${d.getMonth()+1}/${(d.getFullYear()+543).toString().slice(-2)}`;
  };

  let rowsHtml = '';
  const maxRows = 28; // บังคับให้สร้าง 28 แถวเสมอเพื่อให้ตารางเต็มหน้า A4
  for (let i = 0; i < maxRows; i++) {
    const log = logs[i];
    if (log) {
      rowsHtml += `
        <tr style="height: 31px;">
          <td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;">${i + 1}</td>
          <td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;">${formatDateThai(log.date)}</td>
          <td style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: left; padding-left: 8px;">${log.surahName || ''}</td>
          <td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;">${log.logType === 'سبق' ? '✓' : ''}</td>
          <td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;">${log.logType === 'سبقي' ? '✓' : ''}</td>
          <td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;">${log.logType === 'منزل' ? '✓' : ''}</td>
          <td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;"></td>
        </tr>
      `;
    } else {
      // แถวว่าง (บังคับความสูง 31px เพื่อดันให้เต็มหน้า)
      rowsHtml += `<tr style="height: 31px;"><td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;">${i + 1}</td><td style="border: 1px solid #000;"></td><td style="border: 1px solid #000;"></td><td style="border: 1px solid #000;"></td><td style="border: 1px solid #000;"></td><td style="border: 1px solid #000;"></td><td style="border: 1px solid #000;"></td></tr>`;
    }
  }

  return `
<div class="page">
  <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 10px; font-size: 18px; font-weight: bold;">
    บันทึกการท่องจำอัลกุรอานประจำวัน / <span class="arabic" style="font-weight: normal; font-size: 22px;">جاتتن حفظ القرآن هارين</span>
  </div>
  
  <div style="font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 5px;">
    ชื่อ: <strong>${s.firstName} ${s.lastName}</strong> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ชั้น: <strong>${s.classRoom}</strong> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; รหัส: <strong>${s.studentId}</strong>
  </div>

  <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 13px;">
    <thead>
      <tr>
        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 6%; padding: 4px; text-align: center;">ที่<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:12px;">بيلاغن</span></th>
        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 14%; padding: 4px; text-align: center;">วัน เดือน ปี<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:12px;">تاريخ</span></th>
        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 28%; padding: 4px; text-align: center;">ซูเราะห์<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:12px;">سورة</span></th>
        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 10%; padding: 4px; text-align: center;">ท่องจำ<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:12px;">سبق</span></th>
        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 12%; padding: 4px; text-align: center;">ทบทวนใหม่<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:12px;">سبقي</span></th>
        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 12%; padding: 4px; text-align: center;">ทบทวนเก่า<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:12px;">منزل</span></th>
        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 18%; padding: 4px; text-align: center;">ลงชื่อผู้สอน<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:12px;">تندا تاغن فغاجر</span></th>
      </tr>
    </thead>
    <tbody>${rowsHtml}</tbody>
  </table>
</div>
  `;
}

// ============================================
// 📊 Summary Page (บังคับขึ้นหน้าใหม่เสมอ + ดึงเดือนจากตั้งค่า)
// ============================================
function buildSummaryPage(r, schoolName, currentTerm) {
  const teacher = r.teacher;
  const monthly = r.monthlyStats || {};
  const currentYear = currentTerm.split('/')[1] || '2569';

  // 📥 ดึงข้อมูลเดือนจาก appSettings (ถ้าไม่มีให้ใช้ค่าเริ่มต้น)
  const term1MonthsThai = (appSettings.term1MonthsThai || 'พ.ค., มิ.ย., ก.ค., ส.ค., ก.ย., ต.ค.').split(',').map(s=>s.trim());
  const term1MonthsArabic = (appSettings.term1MonthsArabic || 'فرسفاكوم, ميتونايون, كراكداكوم, سيغهاكوم, كنيابون, تولاكوم').split(',').map(s=>s.trim());
  const term2MonthsThai = (appSettings.term2MonthsThai || 'พ.ย., ธ.ค., ม.ค., ก.พ., มี.ค., เม.ย.').split(',').map(s=>s.trim());
  const term2MonthsArabic = (appSettings.term2MonthsArabic || 'فرسجيكاكون, تنواكوم, مكراكوم, كومفافن, ميناكوم, أبريل').split(',').map(s=>s.trim());

  const gradingCriteria = [
    'เกณฑ์การประเมินให้ระดับผลการ',
    'ท่องจำอัลกุรอานมีดังนี้',
    'เกรด 4 &nbsp; คะแนน 80-100 = 5 แผ่น',
    'เกรด 3.5 คะแนน 75-79 &nbsp;= 4 แผ่น',
    'เกรด 3 &nbsp; คะแนน 70-74 &nbsp;= 3.5 แผ่น',
    'เกรด 2.5 คะแนน 65-69 &nbsp;= 3 แผ่น',
    'เกรด 2 &nbsp; คะแนน 60-64 &nbsp;= 2.5 แผ่น',
    'เกรด 1.5 คะแนน 55-59 &nbsp;= 2 แผ่น',
    'เกรด 1 &nbsp; คะแนน 50-54 &nbsp;= 1 แผ่น'
  ];

  const getMonthData = (monthNum, year) => monthly[`${year}-${String(monthNum).padStart(2, '0')}`] || null;

  const buildTermTable = (termNum, thaiMonths, arabicMonths) => {
    const thaiYear = parseInt(currentYear);
    const startMonth = termNum === 1 ? 5 : 11;
    let totalPages = 0;
    let rows = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const thaiMonth = thaiMonths[i];
      let mData = null;

      if (thaiMonth) {
        let monthNum = startMonth + i;
        let monthYear = thaiYear - 543;
        if (monthNum > 12) { monthNum -= 12; monthYear += 1; }
        mData = getMonthData(monthNum, monthYear);
        if (mData) totalPages += mData.totalPages;
      }

      const checks = [4, 3.5, 3, 2.5, 2, 1.5, 1].map(g => 
        `<td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;">${(mData && mData.gradeCount[g] > 0) ? '/' : ''}</td>`
      ).join('');

      const noteText = gradingCriteria[i] || '';
      
      rows += `
        <tr>
          <td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000; height: 19px;">${thaiMonth ? (i + 1) : ''}</td>
          <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding-left: 5px; text-align: left;">${thaiMonth ? `${thaiMonth} / <span class="arabic">${arabicMonths[i]}</span>` : ''}</td>
          <td class="center-text" style="border: 1px solid #000;">${mData ? mData.totalPages.toFixed(1) : ''}</td>
          ${checks}
          <td style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: left; padding-left: 8px; font-size: 11px;">${noteText}</td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }

    return `
      <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 3px;">
          <span>ผลการประเมินการท่องจำอัลกุรอาน ภาคเรียนที่ ${termNum} ปีการศึกษา ${currentYear}</span>
          <span class="arabic" style="font-weight: normal; font-size: 16px;">حاصيل فنيلاين حفظ القرآن فغكل ${termNum} تاهون</span>
        </div>
        <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 12px;">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th rowspan="2" style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 4%; text-align: center;">ที่<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:10px;">بيلاغن</span></th>
              <th rowspan="2" style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 14%; text-align: center;">เดือน<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:10px;">بولن</span></th>
              <th rowspan="2" style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 10%; text-align: center;">จำนวนแผ่น<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:10px;">جمله لمبر</span></th>
              <th colspan="7" style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 35%; text-align: center;">ระดับผลการประเมิน<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:10px;">فريكسا فنيلاين</span></th>
              <th rowspan="2" style="border: 1px solid #000; width: 37%; text-align: center;">หมายเหตุ<br><span class="arabic" style="font-weight:normal; font-size:10px;">ملاحظة</span></th>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <th style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: center;">4</th><th style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: center;">3.5</th><th style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: center;">3</th><th style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: center;">2.5</th><th style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: center;">2</th><th style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: center;">1.5</th><th style="border: 1px solid #000; text-align: center;">1</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
        </table>
        <div style="font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.5; margin-top: 5px;">
          <div style="display: flex;"><div style="width: 150px;">จำนวนยุซที่ได้ ....................</div><div class="arabic">جمله جزء يغدافت</div></div>
          <div style="display: flex;"><div style="width: 150px;">จำนวนแผ่นที่ได้ ...................</div><div class="arabic">جمله لمبر يغدافت</div></div>
          <div style="display: flex; border-bottom: 1px solid #000; padding-bottom: 5px;"><div style="width: 150px;">ผลการประเมิน ......................</div><div class="arabic">حاصيل فنيلاين</div></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  };

  return `
<div class="page page-summary" style="padding: 10px;">
  ${buildTermTable(1, term1MonthsThai, term1MonthsArabic)}
  ${buildTermTable(2, term2MonthsThai, term2MonthsArabic)}

  <div style="margin-top: 20px; display: flex; justify-content: space-around; font-size: 13px; text-align: center;">
    <div>
      <div style="margin-top: 30px;">ลงชื่อ ................................................</div>
      <div style="margin-top: 5px;">(${teacher ? teacher.firstName + ' ' + teacher.lastName : '................................................'})</div>
      <div style="margin-top: 2px;">ครูที่ปรึกษา</div>
    </div>
    <div>
      <div style="margin-top: 30px;">ลงชื่อ ................................................</div>
      <div style="margin-top: 5px;">(................................................)</div>
      <div style="margin-top: 2px;">ผู้อำนวยการ</div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
  `;
}

// ============================================
// 📝 Notes Page (บันทึกเพิ่มเติม - ร่องรอยคอมเมนต์ครู)
// ============================================
function buildNotesPage(r, schoolName) {
  const s = r.student;
  const logs = r.allLogs || [];

  // กรองเฉพาะ log ที่มี note (ความคิดเห็นครู)
  const notesLogs = logs.filter(l => l.note && String(l.note).trim() !== '');

  const formatDateThai = (dateStr) => {
    if (!dateStr) return '';
    const d = new Date(dateStr);
    return `${d.getDate()}/${d.getMonth()+1}/${(d.getFullYear()+543).toString().slice(-2)}`;
  };

  // สร้างเนื้อหาบันทึก (แต่ละ entry ใช้ 2 บรรทัด: header + content)
  // รวมบรรทัดของแถวเปล่าเต็มหน้า A4 (ประมาณ 28 บรรทัด)
  const totalLines = 28;
  let linesHtml = '';
  let linesUsed = 0;

  notesLogs.forEach((log, idx) => {
    if (linesUsed >= totalLines) return;

    // Header line: วันที่ + ประเภท + ซูเราะห์
    const dateText = formatDateThai(log.date);
    const typeText = log.logType || '';
    const surahText = log.surahName ? ` • ${log.surahName}` : '';

    linesHtml += `
      <div class="notebook-line" style="font-weight: 600; color: #000;">
        <span style="color: #666; font-size: 11px; margin-right: 8px;">${idx + 1}.</span>
        ${dateText} — ${typeText}${surahText}
      </div>
    `;
    linesUsed++;

    // Content lines: note text (wrap ข้อความอัตโนมัติ)
    if (linesUsed < totalLines) {
      linesHtml += `
        <div class="notebook-line" style="padding-left: 24px;">
          ${escapeHtml(String(log.note))}
        </div>
      `;
      linesUsed++;
    }

    // แทรกบรรทัดว่าง 1 บรรทัดระหว่าง entries
    if (linesUsed < totalLines) {
      linesHtml += `<div class="notebook-line"></div>`;
      linesUsed++;
    }
  });

  // เติมบรรทัดว่างให้เต็มหน้า (ครูเขียนต่อด้วยมือได้)
  while (linesUsed < totalLines) {
    linesHtml += `<div class="notebook-line"></div>`;
    linesUsed++;
  }

  return `
<div class="page">

  <!-- หัวเรื่องกลางหน้า -->
  <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px; padding-bottom: 8px; border-bottom: 2px solid #000;">
    <h2 style="font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">บันทึกเพิ่มเติม</h2>
    <p style="font-size: 12px; color: #666; margin-top: 4px;">
      ชื่อ: <strong>${s.firstName} ${s.lastName}</strong> &nbsp;|&nbsp;
      ชั้น: <strong>${s.classRoom}</strong> &nbsp;|&nbsp;
      รหัส: <strong>${s.studentId}</strong>
    </p>
  </div>

  <!-- สมุดมีเส้น -->
  <div class="notebook-paper">
    ${linesHtml}
  </div>

  <div style="margin-top: 8px; font-size: 10px; color: #555; text-align: right;">
    พิมพ์เมื่อ: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('th-TH')}
  </div>

</div>
  `;
}

// Helper: escape HTML เพื่อกัน XSS ในข้อความ note
function escapeHtml(text) {
  if (!text) return '';
  return String(text)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
}


// ============================================
// 🖨️ Bulk Print System - พิมพ์ทั้งห้อง
// ============================================
function showBulkPrintModal() {
  // ดึงรายการห้องที่มีอยู่จาก allStudents
  const uniqueClasses = [...new Set(allStudents.map(s => s.classRoom).filter(c => c))].sort();
  
  if (uniqueClasses.length === 0) {
    showToast('ไม่มีข้อมูลห้องเรียน', 'warning');
    return;
  }
  
  document.getElementById('modalContainer').innerHTML = `
    <div class="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 fade-in" onclick="if(event.target === this) closeModal()">
      <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-lg max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div class="p-5 border-b flex items-center justify-between sticky top-0 bg-white">
          <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">🖨️ พิมพ์รายงาน</h3>
          <button onclick="closeModal()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700">
            <svg class="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"/></svg>
          </button>
        </div>
        
        <div class="p-5 space-y-5">
          
          <!-- Step 1: เลือกห้อง -->
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">
              📚 Step 1: เลือกห้องเรียน
            </label>
            <select id="bulkClassSelect" class="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500">
              <option value="">-- เลือกห้องเรียน --</option>
              ${uniqueClasses.map(c => {
                const count = allStudents.filter(s => s.classRoom === c).length;
                return `<option value="${c}">${c} (${count} คน)</option>`;
              }).join('')}
              <option value="__ALL__" style="font-weight: bold; color: #2E7D32;">
                🏫 ทุกห้อง (${allStudents.length} คน)
              </option>
            </select>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Step 2: เลือกประเภทรายงาน -->
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">
              📋 Step 2: เลือกประเภทรายงาน
            </label>
            <div class="space-y-2">
              <label class="flex items-center p-3 border-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-50">
                <input type="radio" name="bulkReportType" value="full" checked class="mr-3">
                <div class="flex-1">
                  <p class="font-medium text-gray-800">📚 รายงานฉบับเต็ม</p>
                  <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">หน้าปก + บันทึก + สรุป (ยาว 4 หน้าต่อคน)</p>
                </div>
              </label>
              
              <label class="flex items-center p-3 border-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-50">
                <input type="radio" name="bulkReportType" value="cover" class="mr-3">
                <div class="flex-1">
                  <p class="font-medium text-gray-800">📄 หน้าปก</p>
                  <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">เฉพาะหน้าปกเอกสาร (1 หน้าต่อคน)</p>
                </div>
              </label>
              
              <label class="flex items-center p-3 border-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-50">
                <input type="radio" name="bulkReportType" value="daily" class="mr-3">
                <div class="flex-1">
                  <p class="font-medium text-gray-800">📝 บันทึกประจำวัน</p>
                  <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">ตารางบันทึกรายวัน (1 หน้าต่อคน)</p>
                </div>
              </label>
              
              <label class="flex items-center p-3 border-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-50">
                <input type="radio" name="bulkReportType" value="summary" class="mr-3">
                <div class="flex-1">
                  <p class="font-medium text-gray-800">📊 สรุปภาคเรียน</p>
                  <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">ตารางสรุปผลประเมิน (1 หน้าต่อคน)</p>
                </div>
              </label>

              <label class="flex items-center p-3 border-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-50">
                <input type="radio" name="bulkReportType" value="notes" class="mr-3">
                <div class="flex-1">
                  <p class="font-medium text-gray-800">📓 บันทึกเพิ่มเติม</p>
                  <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">หน้าสมุดมีเส้น พร้อมร่องรอยความคิดเห็นครู (1 หน้าต่อคน)</p>
                </div>
              </label>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <!-- ข้อมูลเตือน -->
          <div id="bulkWarning" class="hidden p-3 bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-200 rounded-lg">
            <p class="text-xs text-yellow-800">
              ⚠️ <strong>คำแนะนำ:</strong> หากมีนักเรียนจำนวนมาก การสร้างรายงานอาจใช้เวลาสักครู่ โปรดรอจนกว่าหน้าต่าง Print จะเปิดขึ้น
            </p>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Preview Count -->
          <div id="bulkPreview" class="hidden p-3 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg">
            <p class="text-sm text-blue-900">
              📊 จะพิมพ์: <strong id="previewCount">0</strong> คน = 
              <strong id="previewPages">0</strong> หน้ากระดาษ
            </p>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Action Buttons -->
          <div class="flex gap-3 pt-3 border-t">
            <button onclick="closeModal()" 
              class="flex-1 bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 py-3 rounded-lg font-medium">
              ยกเลิก
            </button>
            <button onclick="executeBulkPrint()" 
              class="flex-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-medium shadow">
              🖨️ เริ่มพิมพ์
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  
  // Update preview เมื่อเลือก
  document.getElementById('bulkClassSelect').addEventListener('change', updateBulkPreview);
  document.querySelectorAll('input[name="bulkReportType"]').forEach(radio => {
    radio.addEventListener('change', updateBulkPreview);
  });
}

function updateBulkPreview() {
  const classSelect = document.getElementById('bulkClassSelect').value;
  const typeEl = document.querySelector('input[name="bulkReportType"]:checked');
  const preview = document.getElementById('bulkPreview');
  const warning = document.getElementById('bulkWarning');
  
  if (!classSelect || !typeEl) {
    preview.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }
  
  let studentCount = 0;
  if (classSelect === '__ALL__') {
    studentCount = allStudents.length;
  } else {
    studentCount = allStudents.filter(s => s.classRoom === classSelect).length;
  }
  
  const pagesPerStudent = typeEl.value === 'full' ? 3 : 1;
  const totalPages = studentCount * pagesPerStudent;
  
  document.getElementById('previewCount').textContent = studentCount;
  document.getElementById('previewPages').textContent = totalPages;
  preview.classList.remove('hidden');
  
  // แสดง warning ถ้ามากกว่า 20 คน
  if (studentCount > 20) {
    warning.classList.remove('hidden');
  } else {
    warning.classList.add('hidden');
  }
}

// ============================================
// 🖨️ เริ่มพิมพ์รายงานหลายคน (ระบบ Supabase)
// ============================================
function executeBulkPrint() {
  const classSelect = document.getElementById('bulkClassSelect').value;
  const typeEl = document.querySelector('input[name="bulkReportType"]:checked');
  
  if (!classSelect || !typeEl) {
    showToast('กรุณาเลือกตัวเลือกให้ครบถ้วน', 'warning');
    return;
  }
  
  let targetStudents = [];
  if (classSelect === '__ALL__') {
    targetStudents = allStudents;
  } else {
    targetStudents = allStudents.filter(s => s.classRoom === classSelect);
  }
  
  if (targetStudents.length === 0) {
    showToast('ไม่มีนักเรียนในห้องนี้', 'warning');
    return;
  }
  
  closeModal();
  bulkGenerateReports(targetStudents, typeEl.value);
}

async function bulkGenerateReports(students, type) {
  const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
  
  if (!printWindow) {
    showToast('กรุณาอนุญาต Popup ในเบราว์เซอร์แล้วลองใหม่', 'error');
    return;
  }
  
  printWindow.document.write(`
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="th">
    <head>
      <meta charset="UTF-8">
      <title>กำลังเตรียมรายงาน...</title>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Sarabun:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
      <style>
        * { font-family: 'Sarabun', sans-serif; margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
        body { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; min-height: 100vh; background: #f5f5f5; }
        .container { text-align: center; padding: 40px; background: white; border-radius: 16px; box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); max-width: 400px; }
        .spinner { width: 60px; height: 60px; border: 4px solid #e0e0e0; border-top-color: #2E7D32; border-radius: 50%; animation: spin 1s linear infinite; margin: 0 auto 20px; }
        @keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
        h2 { color: #333; margin-bottom: 10px; font-size: 20px; }
        p { color: #666; font-size: 14px; }
        .progress-bar { background: #e0e0e0; border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden; margin: 20px 0 10px; height: 8px; }
        .progress-fill { background: #2E7D32; height: 100%; width: 0%; transition: width 0.3s; }
        .count { font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold; color: #2E7D32; margin: 10px 0; }
      </style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="container">
        <div class="spinner"></div>
        <h2>🖨️ กำลังเตรียมรายงาน</h2>
        <div class="count"><span id="current">0</span> / ${students.length} คน</div>
        <p id="statusText">กำลังโหลดข้อมูลจาก Supabase...</p>
        <div class="progress-bar">
          <div class="progress-fill" id="progressFill"></div>
        </div>
        <p style="font-size: 12px; color: #999; margin-top: 15px;">
          ⏳ กรุณารอสักครู่ อย่าปิดหน้าต่างนี้
        </p>
      </div>
    </body>
    </html>
  `);
  
  showLoading(`กำลังเตรียมรายงาน 0/${students.length} คน...`);
  const allReports = [];
  
  for (let i = 0; i < students.length; i++) {
    const student = students[i];
    const progress = Math.round(((i + 1) / students.length) * 100);
    
    document.getElementById('loadingText').textContent = `กำลังเตรียมรายงาน ${i + 1}/${students.length} คน... (${student.firstName})`;
    
    try {
      const currentEl = printWindow.document.getElementById('current');
      const statusEl = printWindow.document.getElementById('statusText');
      const progressEl = printWindow.document.getElementById('progressFill');
      if (currentEl) currentEl.textContent = (i + 1);
      if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = `กำลังคำนวณข้อมูล: ${student.firstName} ${student.lastName}`;
      if (progressEl) progressEl.style.width = progress + '%';
    } catch (e) {}
    
    try {
      const reportData = await fetchReportFromSupabase(student.studentId);
      const teacher = allTeachers.find(t => t.teacherId === reportData.student.teacherId) || null;
      reportData.teacher = teacher;
      allReports.push(reportData);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error loading report for', student.studentId, error);
    }
  }
  
  hideLoading();
  
  if (allReports.length === 0) {
    showToast('ไม่สามารถโหลดข้อมูลได้', 'error');
    try { printWindow.close(); } catch(e) {}
    return;
  }
  
  if (printWindow.closed) {
    showToast('หน้าต่าง Print ถูกปิด', 'error');
    return;
  }
  
  const html = buildBulkReportHTML(allReports, type);
  printWindow.document.open();
  printWindow.document.write(html);
  printWindow.document.close();
  
  showToast(`พร้อมพิมพ์ ${allReports.length} รายงาน`, 'success');
}

// ============================================
// 📄 Bulk Report HTML Builder (พิมพ์รวมหลายคน)
// ============================================
function buildBulkReportHTML(reports, type) {
  const schoolName       = appSettings.schoolName       || 'โรงเรียน';
  const schoolNameEn     = appSettings.schoolNameEn     || '';
  const schoolNameArabic = appSettings.schoolNameArabic || '';
  const logoUrl          = appSettings.logoUrl          || '';
  const hadithArabic     = appSettings.hadithArabic     || 'خَيْرُكُمْ مَنْ تَعَلَّمَ الْقُرْآنَ وَعَلَّمَهُ';
  const hadithEnglish    = appSettings.hadithEnglish    || 'Verily the most superior amongst you (Muslims) are those who learn the Quran & teach it.';
  const reportTitle       = appSettings.reportTitle       || 'แบบบันทึกการท่องจำอัลกุรอาน';
  const reportTitleArabic = appSettings.reportTitleArabic || 'جاتتن حفظ القرآن';
  const programSubtitle   = appSettings.programSubtitle   || 'Al-Quran Program';
  const currentTerm = appSettings.currentTerm || '1/2569';
  const currentYear = currentTerm.split('/')[1] || '2569';

  // 🆕 Cover icon config
  const coverConfig = {
    type:  appSettings.coverIconType  || 'image',
    emoji: appSettings.coverIconEmoji || '📖',
    url:   appSettings.coverImageUrl  || ''
  };

  // ✅ ใช้ CSS เดียวกับ buildReportHTML เพื่อความ consistent
  const baseCSS = `
    * { box-sizing: border-box; }
    body { font-family: 'Sarabun', sans-serif; color: #000; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.3; background: #fff; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    @page { size: A4; margin: 10mm; }

    .page { page-break-after: always; break-after: page; min-height: 275mm; padding: 10px; position: relative; }
    .page-summary { page-break-before: always; break-before: page; }
    .page:last-child { page-break-after: auto; break-after: auto; }

    .arabic { font-family: 'Scheherazade New', 'Traditional Arabic', serif; direction: rtl; }
    table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse !important; margin-bottom: 10px; }
    th, td { border: 1px solid #000 !important; padding: 3px 5px; text-align: center; vertical-align: middle; }
    th { font-weight: bold; }

    .center-text { text-align: center !important; font-weight: bold; }

    @media print {
      body { -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact !important; print-color-adjust: exact !important; }
      .page { box-shadow: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    }

    /* ========== Notebook lined paper ========== */
    .notebook-paper {
      border: 1px solid #000;
      padding: 0;
      min-height: 240mm;
      background: #fff;
    }
    .notebook-line {
      border-bottom: 1px solid #aaa;
      padding: 6px 12px 6px 48px;
      min-height: 28px;
      font-size: 14px;
      line-height: 1.4;
      word-wrap: break-word;
      background: linear-gradient(to right,
        transparent 0, transparent 38px,
        #c00 38px, #c00 39px,
        transparent 39px);
    }
    .notebook-line:first-child {
      border-top: 2px solid #c00;
    }
  `;

  let content = '';

  // สร้างรายงานของแต่ละคน
  reports.forEach((r) => {
    if (type === 'full' || type === 'cover') {
      content += buildCoverPage(
        r.student, r.program, r.teacher,
        schoolName, schoolNameEn, schoolNameArabic,
        logoUrl, coverConfig,
        hadithArabic, hadithEnglish,
        reportTitle, reportTitleArabic, programSubtitle,
        currentYear
      );
    }
    if (type === 'full' || type === 'daily') {
      content += buildDailyLogPage(r, schoolName);
    }
    if (type === 'full' || type === 'summary') {
      content += buildSummaryPage(r, schoolName, currentTerm);
    }
    if (type === 'full' || type === 'notes') {
      content += buildNotesPage(r, schoolName);
    }
  });

  return `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="th">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>รายงานรวม - ${reports.length} คน</title>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Sarabun:wght@300;400;600;700&family=Scheherazade+New:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
  <style>${baseCSS}</style>
</head>
<body>
${content}
<script>
  window.onload = function() { setTimeout(function() { window.print(); }, 1500); };
<\/script>
</body>
</html>
  `;
}

// ============================================
// 📝 Logs Tab State
// ============================================
let allLogs = [];                    // เก็บ logs ดิบใน memory
let logsSearchQuery = '';
let logsClassFilter = localStorage.getItem('hifzLogsClassFilter') || '';
let logsTeacherFilter = localStorage.getItem('hifzLogsTeacherFilter') || '';
let logsDateFilter = localStorage.getItem('hifzLogsDateFilter') || 'all'; // all | today | 7days | 30days | thismonth

// ============================================
// 📝 Logs Tab (All Logs View)
// ============================================
function renderLogsTab() {
  const content = document.getElementById('mainContent');
  
  // ⚡ แก้ไข: แยก Cache Key ของผู้ปกครองออกจากส่วนกลางให้ชัดเจน
  let cacheKey = 'logs_all';
  let stdCacheKey = 'students_all';

  if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') {
    cacheKey = `logs_teacher_${currentUser.userId}`;
    stdCacheKey = `students_teacher_${currentUser.userId}`;
  } else if (currentUser.role === 'parent') {
    cacheKey = `logs_parent_${currentUser.userId}`;
    stdCacheKey = `students_parent_${currentUser.userId}`;
  }

  const cachedLogs = cacheGet(cacheKey);
  const cachedStudents = cacheGet(stdCacheKey);

  // ⚡ มี cache ครบ → แสดงทันที + refresh background
  if (cachedLogs && cachedStudents) {
    allStudents = cachedStudents.students;
    displayLogs(cachedLogs.logs);
    refreshLogsData(cacheKey, true); // silent refresh
    return;
  }

  const loader = showProgressLoader({ estimatedSeconds: 10 });
  refreshLogsData(cacheKey, false, loader);
}

// ============================================
// 📝 โหลดข้อมูลบันทึกการท่อง (เวอร์ชัน Supabase)
// ============================================
async function refreshLogsData(cacheKey, silent, loader) {
  try {
    // 1. ดึงนักเรียนตาม Role
    let stdQuery = supabaseClient.from('Students').select('*').eq('isActive', true);
    if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') stdQuery = stdQuery.eq('teacherId', currentUser.userId);
    if (currentUser.role === 'parent') stdQuery = stdQuery.eq('parentId', currentUser.userId);
    
    // วิ่งไปดึงข้อมูลพร้อมกัน ⚡
    const [stdRes, tchRes] = await Promise.all([
      stdQuery,
      supabaseClient.from('Teachers').select('*').eq('isActive', true)
    ]);

    if (stdRes.error) throw stdRes.error;
    if (tchRes.error) throw tchRes.error;

    const students = stdRes.data || [];

    // 2. สร้างเงื่อนไขการดึง Logs
    let logsQuery = supabaseClient
      .from('Logs')
      .select('*')
      .order('date', { ascending: false })
      .limit(500);

    if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') {
      logsQuery = logsQuery.eq('teacherId', currentUser.userId);
    } else if (currentUser.role === 'parent') {
      // 👈 ถ้าเป็นผู้ปกครอง ต้องเอา id ลูกไปกรองดึง Log
      const sIds = students.map(s => s.studentId);
      if (sIds.length > 0) {
         logsQuery = logsQuery.in('studentId', sIds);
      } else {
         logsQuery = logsQuery.eq('studentId', 'NONE');
      }
    }

    const logsRes = await logsQuery;
    if (logsRes.error) throw logsRes.error;

    allLogs = logsRes.data || [];
    allStudents = students;
    allTeachers = tchRes.data || [];

    // 3. อัปเดต Cache ให้ตรงกับ Role
    cacheSet(cacheKey, { success: true, logs: allLogs });

    // (เก็บ Cache นักเรียนและครูเผื่อแท็บอื่นเรียกใช้)
    let stCacheKey = 'students_all';
    if (currentUser.role === 'teacher') stCacheKey = `students_teacher_${currentUser.userId}`;
    else if (currentUser.role === 'parent') stCacheKey = `students_parent_${currentUser.userId}`;

    cacheSet(stCacheKey, { success: true, students: allStudents });
    cacheSet('teachers', { success: true, teachers: allTeachers });

    // 4. ส่งข้อมูลไปวาดลงตาราง
    if (loader) {
      loader.finish();
      setTimeout(function() {
        if (currentTab === 'logs') displayLogs(allLogs);
      }, 500);
    } else {
      if (!silent && currentTab === 'logs') displayLogs(allLogs);
    }

  } catch (error) {
    if (loader) loader.fail('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + error.message);
    else if (!silent) showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + error.message, 'error');
    console.error("Fetch Logs Error:", error);
  }
}

// ตัว orchestrator: แค่เรียก UI renderer
function displayLogs(logs) {
  // ถ้าถูกส่ง logs มา (จากการโหลดครั้งแรก) เก็บใน memory
  if (logs) allLogs = logs;
  renderLogsUI();
}

function renderLogsUI() {
  // Filter ใน memory
  let filtered = allLogs;

  // 🔍 Search
  if (logsSearchQuery) {
    const q = logsSearchQuery.toLowerCase();
    filtered = filtered.filter(log => {
      const student = allStudents.find(s => s.studentId === log.studentId);
      const teacher = allTeachers.find(t => t.teacherId === log.teacherId);
      const studentName = student ? (student.firstName + ' ' + student.lastName).toLowerCase() : '';
      const teacherName = teacher ? (teacher.firstName + ' ' + teacher.lastName).toLowerCase() : '';

      return studentName.includes(q) ||
        teacherName.includes(q) ||
        String(log.studentId || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(log.surahName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(log.ayah || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(log.note || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(log.logType || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(log.juzNumber || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
  }

  // 🏫 Class filter
  if (logsClassFilter) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(log => {
      const student = allStudents.find(s => s.studentId === log.studentId);
      return student && student.classRoom === logsClassFilter;
    });
  }

  // 👩‍🏫 Teacher filter
  if (logsTeacherFilter) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(log => log.teacherId === logsTeacherFilter);
  }

  // 📅 Date filter
  if (logsDateFilter && logsDateFilter !== 'all') {
    const now = new Date();
    now.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    let cutoff;

    if (logsDateFilter === 'today') {
      cutoff = now;
    } else if (logsDateFilter === '7days') {
      cutoff = new Date(now); cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - 7);
    } else if (logsDateFilter === '30days') {
      cutoff = new Date(now); cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - 30);
    } else if (logsDateFilter === 'thismonth') {
      cutoff = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);
    }

    if (cutoff) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(log => {
        if (!log.date) return false;
        const logDate = new Date(log.date);
        return logDate >= cutoff;
      });
    }
  }

  // สร้าง dropdown options
  const uniqueClasses = [...new Set(allStudents.map(s => s.classRoom).filter(c => c))].sort();
  const teachersList = allTeachers.filter(t => t.role === 'teacher' && t.isActive !== false);
  const showTeacherFilter = currentUser.role === 'admin' || currentUser.role === 'director';

  const hasActiveFilter = logsSearchQuery || logsClassFilter || logsTeacherFilter || (logsDateFilter && logsDateFilter !== 'all');

  const html = `
    <!-- Header -->
    <div class="mb-4">
      <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">📝 บันทึกการท่อง</h2>
      <p class="text-gray-600 text-sm">
        แสดง ${filtered.length} / ${allLogs.length} รายการ
        ${logsSearchQuery ? ` • ค้นหา: "<strong>${logsSearchQuery}</strong>"` : ''}
      </p>
    </div>

    <!-- Toolbar: Search + Filters -->
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4 mb-4">
      <!-- Search -->
      <div class="relative mb-3">
        <svg class="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z"/>
        </svg>
        <input type="text" id="logsSearch"
          placeholder="🔍 ค้นหา ชื่อนักเรียน, ครู, ซูเราะห์, อายะฮ์, บันทึก, ประเภท..."
          value="${logsSearchQuery}"
          class="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600 text-sm">
      </div>

      <!-- Filters Row -->
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">

        <!-- Class Filter -->
        <select id="logsClassFilter"
          class="px-3 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600 text-sm">
          <option value="">🏫 ทุกห้อง</option>
          ${uniqueClasses.map(c => `
            <option value="${c}" ${logsClassFilter === c ? 'selected' : ''}>${c}</option>
          `).join('')}
        </select>

        <!-- Teacher Filter (admin/director only) -->
        ${showTeacherFilter ? `
          <select id="logsTeacherFilter"
            class="px-3 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600 text-sm">
            <option value="">👩‍🏫 ครูทุกคน</option>
            ${teachersList.map(t => `
              <option value="${t.teacherId}" ${logsTeacherFilter === t.teacherId ? 'selected' : ''}>
                ${t.firstName} ${t.lastName}
              </option>
            `).join('')}
          </select>
        ` : ''}

        <!-- Date Filter -->
        <select id="logsDateFilter"
          class="px-3 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600 text-sm">
          <option value="all" ${logsDateFilter === 'all' ? 'selected' : ''}>📅 ทั้งหมด</option>
          <option value="today" ${logsDateFilter === 'today' ? 'selected' : ''}>วันนี้</option>
          <option value="7days" ${logsDateFilter === '7days' ? 'selected' : ''}>7 วันล่าสุด</option>
          <option value="30days" ${logsDateFilter === '30days' ? 'selected' : ''}>30 วันล่าสุด</option>
          <option value="thismonth" ${logsDateFilter === 'thismonth' ? 'selected' : ''}>เดือนนี้</option>
        </select>

        <!-- Clear -->
        ${hasActiveFilter ? `
          <button onclick="clearLogsFilters()" class="text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 text-sm font-medium ml-auto">
            ✕ ล้างตัวกรอง
          </button>
        ` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Content -->
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow">
      <div class="divide-y">
        ${filtered.length === 0 ? renderNoLogs() :
          filtered.map(log => {
            const info = getLogTypeInfo(log.logType);
            const student = allStudents.find(s => s.studentId === log.studentId);
            const teacher = allTeachers.find(t => t.teacherId === log.teacherId);
            return `
              <div class="p-4 hover:bg-gray-50">
                <div class="flex items-start justify-between">
                  <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 flex-wrap">
                      <span class="font-bold text-gray-800">${student ? student.firstName + ' ' + student.lastName : log.studentId}</span>
                      ${student ? `<span class="text-xs text-gray-500">${student.classRoom}</span>` : ''}
                      <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs ${info.color}">${info.label}</span>
                      ${log.status === 'PASS' ? '<span class="text-green-600 text-xs font-medium">✓ ผ่าน</span>' : ''}
                      ${log.status === 'NEED_REVIEW' ? '<span class="text-yellow-600 text-xs font-medium">⚠ ทบทวน</span>' : ''}
                      ${log.status === 'ABSENT' ? '<span class="text-red-600 text-xs font-medium">✗ ขาด</span>' : ''}
                    </div>
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">
                      ${log.surahName ? `ยุซ ${log.juzNumber} - ${log.surahName}` : '-'}
                      ${log.ayah ? ` • ${log.ayah}` : ''}
                      ${log.score > 0 ? ` • ⭐ ${log.score}/5` : ''}
                    </p>
                    ${teacher ? `<p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">👩‍🏫 ${teacher.firstName} ${teacher.lastName}</p>` : ''}
                    ${log.note ? `<p class="text-xs text-gray-500 italic mt-1">"${log.note}"</p>` : ''}
                  </div>
                  <div class="text-right text-xs text-gray-500 ml-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                    ${formatDateTime(log.date)}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join('')
        }
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = html;

  // Event listeners
  const searchEl = document.getElementById('logsSearch');
  if (searchEl) {
    searchEl.addEventListener('input', debounce(function() {
      logsSearchQuery = this.value;
      renderLogsUI();
      setTimeout(() => {
        const el = document.getElementById('logsSearch');
        if (el) {
          el.focus();
          el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length);
        }
      }, 0);
    }, 300));
  }

  const classEl = document.getElementById('logsClassFilter');
  if (classEl) {
    classEl.addEventListener('change', function() {
      logsClassFilter = this.value;
      localStorage.setItem('hifzLogsClassFilter', this.value);
      renderLogsUI();
    });
  }

  const teacherEl = document.getElementById('logsTeacherFilter');
  if (teacherEl) {
    teacherEl.addEventListener('change', function() {
      logsTeacherFilter = this.value;
      localStorage.setItem('hifzLogsTeacherFilter', this.value);
      renderLogsUI();
    });
  }

  const dateEl = document.getElementById('logsDateFilter');
  if (dateEl) {
    dateEl.addEventListener('change', function() {
      logsDateFilter = this.value;
      localStorage.setItem('hifzLogsDateFilter', this.value);
      renderLogsUI();
    });
  }
}

function renderNoLogs() {
  const hasActiveFilter = logsSearchQuery || logsClassFilter || logsTeacherFilter || (logsDateFilter && logsDateFilter !== 'all');
  if (hasActiveFilter) {
    return `
      <div class="p-12 text-center">
        <p class="text-4xl mb-3">🔍</p>
        <p class="text-gray-600 font-medium mb-1">ไม่พบบันทึกที่ตรงกับเงื่อนไข</p>
        <p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">ลองเปลี่ยนคำค้นหาหรือตัวกรอง</p>
        <button onclick="clearLogsFilters()" class="text-blue-600 hover:underline text-sm">
          ล้างตัวกรองทั้งหมด
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  return '<p class="p-12 text-center text-gray-500">ยังไม่มีบันทึก</p>';
}

function clearLogsFilters() {
  logsSearchQuery = '';
  logsClassFilter = '';
  logsTeacherFilter = '';
  logsDateFilter = 'all';
  localStorage.removeItem('hifzLogsClassFilter');
  localStorage.removeItem('hifzLogsTeacherFilter');
  localStorage.removeItem('hifzLogsDateFilter');
  renderLogsUI();
}

// ============================================
// 🖼️ Image Upload & Preview Helpers
// ============================================

// อัปโหลดไฟล์เข้า Drive → ได้ URL กลับมา → preview
function handleImageUpload(inputEl, targetType) {
  const file = inputEl.files[0];
  if (!file) return;

  // Validate
  if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
    showToast('กรุณาเลือกไฟล์รูปภาพ (jpg, png, gif)', 'error');
    inputEl.value = '';
    return;
  }
  if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
    showToast('ขนาดไฟล์ต้องไม่เกิน 5MB', 'error');
    inputEl.value = '';
    return;
  }

  // อ่านไฟล์เป็น base64
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) {
    const base64 = e.target.result.split(',')[1];

    showLoading('กำลังอัปโหลด...');
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
        hideLoading();
        inputEl.value = ''; // เคลียร์ input file

        if (res && res.success) {
          // ใส่ URL ลงใน text input + preview
          const urlInput = document.getElementById(
            targetType === 'cover' ? 's_coverImageUrl' : 's_logoUrl'
          );
          if (urlInput) {
            urlInput.value = res.url;
            validateAndPreviewImage(urlInput, targetType);
          }
          showToast('✅ อัปโหลดสำเร็จ', 'success');
        } else {
          showToast((res && res.message) || 'อัปโหลดไม่สำเร็จ', 'error');
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
        hideLoading();
        inputEl.value = '';
        showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + err, 'error');
      })
      .uploadImageToDrive(base64, file.name, file.type);
  };
  reader.onerror = function() {
    showToast('อ่านไฟล์ไม่สำเร็จ', 'error');
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

// ตรวจสอบลิงก์รูป + แสดง preview
function validateAndPreviewImage(inputEl, targetType) {
  const url = (inputEl.value || '').trim();
  const previewEl = document.getElementById(
    targetType === 'cover' ? 'coverImagePreview' : 'logoImagePreview'
  );
  const statusEl = document.getElementById(
    targetType === 'cover' ? 'coverImageStatus' : 'logoImageStatus'
  );

  if (!previewEl || !statusEl) return;

  if (!url) {
    previewEl.innerHTML = '';
    statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-gray-400 text-xs">ยังไม่มีรูป</span>';
    return;
  }

  statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-yellow-600 text-xs">⏳ กำลังตรวจสอบลิงก์...</span>';
  previewEl.innerHTML = '';

  const img = new Image();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(function() {
    img.src = ''; // abort
    statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-red-600 text-xs">❌ โหลดรูปนานเกินไป - อาจใช้งานไม่ได้</span>';
  }, 10000);

  img.onload = function() {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    statusEl.innerHTML = `<span class="text-green-600 text-xs">✓ รูปภาพใช้งานได้ (${img.naturalWidth}×${img.naturalHeight}px)</span>`;
    previewEl.innerHTML = `<img src="${url}" class="max-h-24 object-contain border rounded bg-gray-50 p-2">`;
  };

  img.onerror = function() {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-red-600 text-xs">❌ โหลดรูปภาพไม่ได้ ตรวจสอบลิงก์หรือสิทธิ์การแชร์</span>';
    previewEl.innerHTML = '';
  };

  img.src = url;
}

// ทดสอบ Folder ID
function testFolderAccess() {
  const folderId = document.getElementById('s_uploadFolderId').value.trim();
  const statusEl = document.getElementById('folderTestStatus');

  if (!folderId) {
    statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-red-600 text-xs">กรุณาใส่ Folder ID</span>';
    return;
  }

  statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-yellow-600 text-xs">⏳ กำลังทดสอบ...</span>';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (res && res.success) {
        statusEl.innerHTML = `<span class="text-green-600 text-xs">✓ ${res.message}</span>`;
      } else {
        statusEl.innerHTML = `<span class="text-red-600 text-xs">❌ ${res.message}</span>`;
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      statusEl.innerHTML = `<span class="text-red-600 text-xs">❌ ${err}</span>`;
    })
    .testDriveFolder(folderId);
}

function renderAdminPanel() {
  const html = `
    <div class="mb-6">
      <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">⚙️ จัดการระบบ</h2>
      <p class="text-gray-600 text-sm">เครื่องมือสำหรับผู้ดูแลระบบ</p>
    </div>
    
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow mb-6">
      <div class="flex overflow-x-auto border-b">
        <button onclick="switchAdminTab('settings')" id="admintab-settings" class="admin-tab px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 border-transparent hover:bg-gray-50">
          🏫 ตั้งค่าทั่วไป
        </button>
        <button onclick="switchAdminTab('programs')" id="admintab-programs" class="admin-tab px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 border-transparent hover:bg-gray-50">
          📚 โปรแกรม
        </button>
        <button onclick="switchAdminTab('teachers')" id="admintab-teachers" class="admin-tab px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 border-transparent hover:bg-gray-50">
          👩‍🏫 ครู
        </button>
        <button onclick="switchAdminTab('parents')" id="admintab-parents" class="admin-tab px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 border-transparent hover:bg-gray-50">
          👨‍👩‍👧 ผู้ปกครอง
        </button>
        <button onclick="switchAdminTab('upload')" id="admintab-upload" class="admin-tab px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 border-transparent hover:bg-gray-50">
          📤 นำเข้า CSV
        </button>
        <button onclick="switchAdminTab('system')" id="admintab-system" class="admin-tab px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 border-transparent hover:bg-gray-50">
          🛠️ ระบบ
        </button>
      </div>
      <div id="adminContent" class="p-5"></div>
    </div>
  `;
  
  document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = html;
  switchAdminTab('settings');
}

function switchAdminTab(tab) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.admin-tab').forEach(el => {
    el.style.borderBottomColor = '';
    el.style.color = '';
  });
  const active = document.getElementById('admintab-' + tab);
  if (active) {
    active.style.borderBottomColor = 'var(--primary-color)';
    active.style.color = 'var(--primary-color)';
  }
  
  switch(tab) {
    case 'settings': renderAdminSettings(); break;
    case 'programs': renderAdminPrograms(); break;
    case 'teachers': renderAdminTeachers(); break;
    case 'parents': renderAdminParents(); break;
    case 'upload': renderAdminUpload(); break;
    case 'system': renderAdminSystem(); break;
  }
}

const html = `
<h3 class="font-bold text-lg mb-4">🏫 ข้อมูลโรงเรียน</h3>

<form id="settingsForm" class="space-y-4">

  <!-- ชื่อโรงเรียน: 3 ภาษา -->

    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">💡 ชื่อโรงเรียนจะแสดงบน Header และหน้าปกรายงาน</p>

      <input type="text" id="s_schoolNameEn" value="${appSettings.schoolNameEn || ''}"
        placeholder="เช่น PIRAYA-NAWIN KLONGHIN WITAYA SCHOOL"
        class="w-full px-4 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600">
    </div>

    <div>
      <label class="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">ชื่อโรงเรียน (ภาษาอาหรับ/ยาวี)</label>

    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ชื่อแอป & Footer -->
  <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
    <div>
      <label class="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">ชื่อแอปพลิเคชัน</label>
      <input type="text" id="s_appName" value="${appSettings.appName || ''}"
        placeholder="เช่น Hifz Tracker Pro"
        class="w-full px-4 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600">
    </div>
    <div>
      <label class="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">ข้อความ Footer</label>
      <input type="text" id="s_footerText" value="${appSettings.footerText || ''}"
        placeholder="เช่น Powered by Hifz Tracker Pro"
        class="w-full px-4 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600">
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ที่อยู่ -->
  <div>
    <label class="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">ที่อยู่โรงเรียน</label>
    <textarea id="s_address" rows="2"
      placeholder="เช่น 123 ถนนหลัก ตำบลในเมือง..."
      class="w-full px-4 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600">${appSettings.schoolAddress || ''}</textarea>
  </div>

  <!-- เบอร์ & เทอม -->
  <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
    <div>
      <label class="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">เบอร์ติดต่อ</label>
      <input type="text" id="s_phone" value="${appSettings.schoolPhone || ''}"
        placeholder="02-123-4567"
        class="w-full px-4 py-2 border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-green-600">

});

document.getElementById('s_primaryColor').value = this.value;
});
document.getElementById('s_secondaryColor').addEventListener('input', function() {
document.getElementById('s_secondaryColorText').value = this.value;
});

const hadithArabicEl = document.getElementById('s_hadithArabic');
const hadithEnglishEl = document.getElementById('s_hadithEnglish');
const hadithThaiEl = document.getElementById('s_hadithThai');

if (hadithArabicEl) {
hadithArabicEl.addEventListener('input', function() {
document.getElementById('hadithPreviewArabic').textContent = this.value;
});
}
if (hadithEnglishEl) {
hadithEnglishEl.addEventListener('input', function() {
document.getElementById('hadithPreviewEnglish').textContent = '"' + this.value + '"';
});
}
if (hadithThaiEl) {
hadithThaiEl.addEventListener('input', function() {
document.getElementById('hadithPreviewThai').textContent = this.value;
});
}


document.getElementById('settingsForm').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
e.preventDefault();


// รวมค่า checkbox ของประเภทที่ต้องบันทึก pages
const selectedLogTypes = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.log-type-pages:checked'))
.map(cb => cb.value).join(',');

const newSettings = {
  // 🏫 ข้อมูลโรงเรียน
  schoolName:        document.getElementById('s_schoolName') ? document.getElementById('s_schoolName').value : '',
  schoolNameEn:      document.getElementById('s_schoolNameEn') ? document.getElementById('s_schoolNameEn').value : '',
  schoolNameArabic:  document.getElementById('s_schoolNameArabic') ? document.getElementById('s_schoolNameArabic').value : '',
  appName:           document.getElementById('s_appName') ? document.getElementById('s_appName').value : '',

  // 📍 ที่อยู่ / เบอร์  (ID: s_address, s_phone — ไม่ใช่ s_schoolAddress, s_schoolPhone)
  schoolAddress: document.getElementById('s_address') ? document.getElementById('s_address').value : '',
  schoolPhone:   document.getElementById('s_phone')   ? document.getElementById('s_phone').value   : '',

  // 📅 เทอม/ปีการศึกษา  (ID: s_term, s_terms — ไม่ใช่ s_currentTerm, s_termsPerYear)
  currentTerm:  document.getElementById('s_term')  ? document.getElementById('s_term').value  : '',
  termsPerYear: document.getElementById('s_terms') ? document.getElementById('s_terms').value : '',

  // 🎨 ธีม
  primaryColor:   document.getElementById('s_primaryColorText') ? document.getElementById('s_primaryColorText').value : '',
  secondaryColor: document.getElementById('s_secondaryColorText') ? document.getElementById('s_secondaryColorText').value : '',

  // 🖼️ โลโก้ & รูปภาพหน้าปก
  logoUrl:        document.getElementById('s_logoUrl') ? document.getElementById('s_logoUrl').value : '',
  coverImageUrl:  document.getElementById('s_coverImageUrl') ? document.getElementById('s_coverImageUrl').value : '',
  coverIconType:  (function() {
    const el = document.querySelector('input[name="s_coverIconType"]:checked');
    return el ? el.value : 'image';
  })(),
  coverIconEmoji: document.getElementById('s_coverIconEmoji') ? document.getElementById('s_coverIconEmoji').value : '📖',

  // 📘 ข้อความบนหน้าปกรายงาน (3 ภาษา)
  reportTitle:       document.getElementById('s_reportTitle') ? document.getElementById('s_reportTitle').value : '',
  reportTitleArabic: document.getElementById('s_reportTitleArabic') ? document.getElementById('s_reportTitleArabic').value : '',
  programSubtitle:   document.getElementById('s_programSubtitle') ? document.getElementById('s_programSubtitle').value : '',

  // 🕌 ฮะดีษ
  hadithArabic:  document.getElementById('s_hadithArabic') ? document.getElementById('s_hadithArabic').value : '',
  hadithEnglish: document.getElementById('s_hadithEnglish') ? document.getElementById('s_hadithEnglish').value : '',
  hadithThai:    document.getElementById('s_hadithThai') ? document.getElementById('s_hadithThai').value : '',

  // 📅 ชื่อเดือนในตารางประเมินผล
  term1MonthsThai:   document.getElementById('s_t1_th') ? document.getElementById('s_t1_th').value : '',
  term1MonthsArabic: document.getElementById('s_t1_ar') ? document.getElementById('s_t1_ar').value : '',
  term2MonthsThai:   document.getElementById('s_t2_th') ? document.getElementById('s_t2_th').value : '',
  term2MonthsArabic: document.getElementById('s_t2_ar') ? document.getElementById('s_t2_ar').value : '',

  loadingMessages: document.getElementById('s_loadingMessages') ? document.getElementById('s_loadingMessages').value : '',
  loadingTip: document.getElementById('s_loadingTip') ? document.getElementById('s_loadingTip').value : '',
  developerFooter: document.getElementById('s_developerFooter') ? document.getElementById('s_developerFooter').value : '',

  // 📝 ประเภทการท่องที่ต้องบันทึกจำนวนแผ่น
  logTypesWithPages: selectedLogTypes,

  // 📁 Google Drive Folder ID
  uploadFolderId: document.getElementById('s_uploadFolderId')
    ? document.getElementById('s_uploadFolderId').value.trim()
    : ''
};

showLoading('กำลังบันทึก...');
    
    try {
      // 1. แปลง Object newSettings ให้เป็น Array รูปแบบ [{key: '...', value: '...'}, ...] สำหรับ Supabase
      const settingsArray = Object.keys(newSettings).map(k => ({
        key: k,
        value: String(newSettings[k])
      }));

      // 2. ใช้คำสั่ง upsert เพื่ออัปเดตหรือสร้างใหม่ โดยอิงจากคอลัมน์ 'key'
      const { error } = await supabaseClient
        .from('Settings')
        .upsert(settingsArray, { onConflict: 'key' });

      if (error) throw error;

      hideLoading();
      Object.assign(appSettings, newSettings);
      applyTheme();
      cacheClearAll();
      showToast('บันทึกการตั้งค่าสำเร็จ', 'success');
      setTimeout(() => renderMainApp(), 500);

    } catch (error) {
      hideLoading();
      showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: ' + error.message, 'error');
      console.error(error);
    }
});
}

function applyHadithTemplate(templateNum) {
  const templates = {
    1: {
      arabic: 'خَيْرُكُمْ مَنْ تَعَلَّمَ الْقُرْآنَ وَعَلَّمَهُ',
      english: 'Verily the most superior amongst you (Muslims) are those who learn the Quran & teach it.',
      thai: 'ผู้ประเสริฐที่สุดในหมู่พวกเจ้า คือผู้ที่เรียนรู้อัลกุรอานและสอนมัน'
    },
    2: {
      arabic: 'مَنْ قَرَأَ حَرْفًا مِنْ كِتَابِ اللَّهِ فَلَهُ بِهِ حَسَنَةٌ وَالْحَسَنَةُ بِعَشْرِ أَمْثَالِهَا',
      english: 'Whoever recites a letter from the Book of Allah, he will have a reward. And that reward will be multiplied by ten.',
      thai: 'ผู้ใดที่อ่านอัลกุรอานเพียงหนึ่งอักษร เขาจะได้ผลบุญหนึ่งประการ และผลบุญนั้นจะถูกคูณด้วยสิบเท่า'
    },
    3: {
      arabic: 'اقْرَأْ وَارْتَقِ وَرَتِّلْ كَمَا كُنْتَ تُرَتِّلُ فِي الدُّنْيَا',
      english: 'Recite and ascend (through the ranks of Paradise) and recite as you used to recite in the world.',
      thai: 'จงอ่านและยกระดับตนเอง และอ่านให้ไพเราะเหมือนที่เจ้าเคยอ่านในโลกนี้'
    }
  };
  
  const t = templates[templateNum];
  if (!t) return;
  
  document.getElementById('s_hadithArabic').value = t.arabic;
  document.getElementById('s_hadithEnglish').value = t.english;
  document.getElementById('s_hadithThai').value = t.thai;
  
  // อัปเดต preview
  document.getElementById('hadithPreviewArabic').textContent = t.arabic;
  document.getElementById('hadithPreviewEnglish').textContent = '"' + t.english + '"';
  document.getElementById('hadithPreviewThai').textContent = t.thai;
  
  showToast('ใช้เทมเพลตสำเร็จ', 'success');
}

// ============================================
// Admin: Programs (โหลดแบบ Supabase)
// ============================================
async function renderAdminPrograms() {
  const content = document.getElementById('adminContent');
  content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-8">กำลังโหลด...</div>';
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseClient.from('Programs').select('*').order('programId');
    if (error) throw error;
    allPrograms = data || [];
    
    content.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h3 class="font-bold text-lg">📚 โปรแกรมการเรียน</h3>
        <button onclick="showProgramModal()" class="primary-bg primary-hover text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium">
          + เพิ่มโปรแกรม
        </button>
      </div>
      <div class="space-y-3">
        ${allPrograms.map(p => `
          <div class="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between">
            <div class="flex-1">
              <p class="font-bold text-gray-800">${p.programName}</p>
              <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">
                รหัส: ${p.programId} • ${p.targetJuzPerTerm > 0 ? `เป้าหมาย ${p.targetJuzPerTerm} ยุซ/เทอม` : 'ไม่มีเกณฑ์การประเมิน'}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex gap-2">
              <button onclick="showProgramModal('${p.programId}')" class="text-blue-600 hover:underline text-sm">แก้ไข</button>
              <button onclick="confirmDelete('โปรแกรม ${p.programName}', () => deleteProgramAction('${p.programId}'))" class="text-red-600 hover:underline text-sm">ลบ</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
    `;
  } catch (err) {
    showToast(err.message, 'error');
  }
}

// ============================================
// Admin: Teachers (โหลดแบบ Supabase)
// ============================================
async function renderAdminTeachers() {
  const content = document.getElementById('adminContent');
  content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-8">กำลังโหลด...</div>';
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseClient.from('Teachers').select('*').order('teacherId');
    if (error) throw error;
    allTeachers = data || [];
    
    content.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h3 class="font-bold text-lg">👩‍🏫 ครูและผู้บริหาร</h3>
        <button onclick="showTeacherModal()" class="primary-bg text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm">+ เพิ่มครู</button>
      </div>
      <div class="overflow-x-auto">
        <table class="w-full">
          <thead class="bg-gray-100">
            <tr>
              <th class="px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm">รหัส</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm">ชื่อ-สกุล</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm">บทบาท</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm">ห้อง</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm">เบอร์</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-sm">สถานะ</th>
              <th class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-sm">จัดการ</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody class="divide-y">
            ${allTeachers.map(t => `
              <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50">
                <td class="px-3 py-2 text-sm">${t.teacherId}</td>
                <td class="px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium">${t.firstName} ${t.lastName}</td>
                <td class="px-3 py-2 text-sm">
                  <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs ${t.role === 'admin' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700' : t.role === 'director' ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700' : 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700'}">${t.role}</span>
                </td>
                <td class="px-3 py-2 text-sm">${t.classRoom || '-'}</td>
                <td class="px-3 py-2 text-sm">${t.phone || '-'}</td>
                <td class="px-3 py-2 text-center">
                  <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs ${t.isActive ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'}">${t.isActive ? 'ใช้งาน' : 'ปิด'}</span>
                </td>
                <td class="px-3 py-2 text-center text-sm">
                  <button onclick="showTeacherModal('${t.teacherId}')" class="text-blue-600 hover:underline">แก้</button>
                  <button onclick="toggleTeacherAction('${t.teacherId}')" class="text-yellow-600 hover:underline ml-2">${t.isActive ? 'ปิด' : 'เปิด'}</button>
                  <button onclick="confirmDelete('ครู ${t.firstName}', () => deleteTeacherAction('${t.teacherId}'))" class="text-red-600 hover:underline ml-2">ลบ</button>
                </td>
              </tr>
            `).join('')}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    `;
  } catch (err) {
    showToast(err.message, 'error');
  }
}

// ============================================
async function renderAdminParents() {
  const content = document.getElementById('adminContent');
  content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-8">กำลังโหลด...</div>';
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseClient.from('Parents').select('*').order('parentId');
    if (error) throw error;
    allParents = data || [];
    
    content.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h3 class="font-bold text-lg">👨‍👩‍👧 ผู้ปกครอง</h3>
        <button onclick="showParentModal()" class="primary-bg text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm">+ เพิ่มผู้ปกครอง</button>
